+  // 5.8 Create form (D11 #13) — item / idea variants. Owns form mechanics ONLY
+  // (disable, clear, error placement); the screen's onSubmit builds the request
+  // body and returns the fetchJson promise. No optimistic UI — a successful create
+  // is reflected only when the WS-invalidation refetch re-renders the list.
+  var PROJECTS = ["Vylo", "Tribe", "Learning", "Other"];   // §3.2 Project enum
+  var PRIORITIES = ["P0", "P1", "P2", "P3"];                // §3.2 priorities
+  var FORM_SPECS = {
+    item: {
+      submitLabel: "Add item",
+      fields: [
+        { name: "title", kind: "text", label: "Title", required: true, placeholder: "Item title" },
+        { name: "project", kind: "select", label: "Project", options: PROJECTS, default: "Vylo" },
+        { name: "priority", kind: "select", label: "Priority", options: PRIORITIES, default: "P3" },
+        { name: "deadline", kind: "date", label: "Deadline" }
+      ]
+    },
+    idea: {
+      submitLabel: "Add idea",
+      fields: [
+        { name: "title", kind: "text", label: "Title", required: true, placeholder: "Idea title" },
+        { name: "project", kind: "select", label: "Project", options: PROJECTS,
+          includeBlank: true, blankLabel: "— no project —", default: "" },
+        { name: "body", kind: "textarea", label: "Body" }
+      ]
+    }
+  };
+
+  function createForm(variant, onSubmit) {
+    var spec = FORM_SPECS[variant];
+    var form = make("form", "create-form");
+    form.setAttribute("data-create", variant);
+    var controls = [];
+    spec.fields.forEach(function (field) {
+      var wrap = make("div", "create-form-field");
+      wrap.appendChild(make("label", "create-form-label", field.label));
+      var control;
+      var reset = "";
+      if (field.kind === "select") {
+        control = make("select", "form-control");
+        if (field.includeBlank) {
+          var blank = make("option", null, field.blankLabel);
+          blank.value = "";
+          control.appendChild(blank);
+        }
+        field.options.forEach(function (opt) {
+          var option = make("option", null, opt);
+          option.value = opt;
+          control.appendChild(option);
+        });
+        reset = field.default !== undefined
+          ? field.default
+          : (field.includeBlank ? "" : field.options[0]);
+        control.value = reset;
+      } else if (field.kind === "textarea") {
+        control = make("textarea", "form-control");
+        control.rows = 4;
+      } else {
+        control = make("input", "form-control");
+        control.type = field.kind === "date" ? "date" : "text";
+        if (field.placeholder) {
+          control.setAttribute("placeholder", field.placeholder);
+        }
+      }
+      control.setAttribute("data-input", field.name);
+      if (field.required) {
+        control.required = true;
+      }
+      wrap.appendChild(control);
+      form.appendChild(wrap);
+      controls.push({ name: field.name, control: control, reset: reset });
+    });
+    var actions = make("div", "field-editor-actions");
+    var submit = make("button", "button button--primary", spec.submitLabel);
+    submit.type = "submit";
+    actions.appendChild(submit);
+    form.appendChild(actions);
+
+    form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
+      e.preventDefault();
+      var prior = form.querySelector(".error-line");
+      if (prior) {
+        prior.parentNode.removeChild(prior);
+      }
+      if (!form.checkValidity()) {
+        form.reportValidity();
+        return;
+      }
+      var values = {};
+      controls.forEach(function (entry) {
+        values[entry.name] = entry.control.value;
+      });
+      submit.disabled = true;
+      Promise.resolve(onSubmit(values)).then(
+        function () {
+          controls.forEach(function (entry) {
+            entry.control.value = entry.reset;
+          });
+        },
+        function (err) {
+          actions.prepend(errorLine(err));
+        }
+      ).then(function () {
+        submit.disabled = false;
+      });
+    });
+    return form;
+  }
+
+  // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
+  // T15 additions (D11 items 7, 8, 9, 10, 16) — Day + Review composites, the
+  // pluggable chat-input registry, and the small state-machine mirrors the two
+  // screens share. Additive: nothing above this line is touched. The server
+  // re-validates every grant (machine.py:75-100); these mirrors only pick which
+  // grant options to OFFER, never gate a write.
+  // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+  // §4.1 linear order + §4.2 tables, mirrored for grant-option math only.
+  var STATE_ORDER = [
+    "needs_success", "needs_approach", "needs_plan",
+    "in_progress", "needs_review", "done"
+  ];
+  var GATING_FIELD = {
+    needs_success: "success",
+    needs_approach: "approach",
+    needs_plan: "plan",
+    in_progress: "result"
+  };
+  var ADVANCE = {
+    needs_success: "needs_approach",
+    needs_approach: "needs_plan",
+    needs_plan: "in_progress",
+    in_progress: "needs_review"
+  };
+
+  // Transient conversation-render state (SPEC §11 / §9 no-store doctrine). Chat
+  // messages have NO server representation (chat/service.py:81-104 stores only the
+  // session key), so this is not a cache of canonical data: losing it on reload is
+  // correct. It survives WS-flush re-renders because scripts are not re-executed —
+  // only route() re-runs, and each re-render rebuilds the panel from these.
+  var chatTranscripts = {};   // entity_id -> [{who: "you"|"planner", text: string}]
+  var chatDrafts = {};        // entity_id -> string (pending, unsent input text)
+  var _atcapSeq = 0;          // per-instance unique radio-group names
+
+  function advanceTarget(state, ceiling) {
+    // Mirror of machine.py:40-48 (+ the ceiling=done special case).
+    if (state === "in_progress" && ceiling === "done") {
+      return "done";
+    }
+    return ADVANCE[state] || null;
+  }
+
+  function gatingField(state) {
+    return GATING_FIELD[state] || null;
+  }
+
+  function quietLine(text) {
+    return make("div", "quiet-line", text);
+  }
+
+  // Shared mutating-button discipline (amendment A3): on click clear any prior
+  // error and disable; on rejection re-enable and surface the error; on SUCCESS
+  // stay disabled — the WS-flush re-render replaces the DOM, and re-enabling would
+  // only reopen the flush-gap double-fire window. Private helper (not exported).
+  function bindMutating(button, container, run) {
+    button.addEventListener("click", function () {
+      var prior = container.querySelector(".error-line");
+      if (prior) {
+        container.removeChild(prior);
+      }
+      button.disabled = true;
+      Promise.resolve(run()).then(
+        function () {},
+        function (err) {
+          button.disabled = false;
+          container.prepend(errorLine(err));
+        }
+      );
+    });
+  }
+
+  // --- the pluggable chat-input source (SPEC §14 audio seam) -----------------
+  // A source is factory(ctx) -> HTMLElement. ctx.submit(text) -> Promise is the
+  // only way a source delivers input (the panel owns transport); ctx.initialText
+  // is the preserved draft; ctx.onInput(text) reports pending text so the panel
+  // can preserve the draft across re-renders.
+  function makeTextInputSource(ctx) {
+    var wrap = make("div", "chat-input");
+    var textarea = make("textarea", "field-editor-input chat-input-text");
+    textarea.setAttribute("data-chat-input", "");
+    textarea.rows = 2;
+    textarea.value = ctx.initialText || "";
+    textarea.addEventListener("input", function () {
+      ctx.onInput(textarea.value);
+    });
+    var send = make("button", "button button--primary", "Send");
+    send.type = "button";
+    send.setAttribute("data-chat-send", "");
+    send.addEventListener("click", function () {
+      var text = textarea.value.trim();
+      if (!text) {
+        return;
+      }
+      send.disabled = true;
+      textarea.value = "";
+      ctx.onInput("");
+      Promise.resolve(ctx.submit(text)).then(
+        function () {
+          send.disabled = false;
+        },
+        function () {
+          send.disabled = false;
+        }
+      );
+    });
+    wrap.appendChild(textarea);
+    wrap.appendChild(send);
+    return wrap;
+  }
+
+  // --- D11 item 8: grant-pair picker -----------------------------------------
+  // getGrant() returns null until BOTH halves are explicitly chosen. The ceiling
+  // options are exactly the states resolve_grant accepts (machine.py:94-99): the
+  // NO_FURTHER sentinel plus every STATE_ORDER state at or beyond newState.
+  function grantPairPicker(newState, onChange) {
+    var wrap = make("div", "grant-picker");
+    var label = make("label", "grant-label");
+    label.appendChild(make("span", null, "how far"));
+    var select = make("select", "grant-ceiling");
+    select.setAttribute("data-grant-ceiling", "");
+    var placeholder = make("option", null, "");
+    placeholder.value = "";
+    placeholder.disabled = true;
+    placeholder.selected = true;
+    placeholder.hidden = true;
+    select.appendChild(placeholder);
+    var noFurther = make("option", null, "No further");
+    noFurther.value = "none";
+    select.appendChild(noFurther);
+    var start = STATE_ORDER.indexOf(newState);
+    if (start < 0) {
+      start = 0;
+    }
+    STATE_ORDER.slice(start).forEach(function (state) {
+      var option = make("option", null, state.replace(/_/g, " "));
+      option.value = state;
+      select.appendChild(option);
+    });
+    select.addEventListener("change", onChange);
+    label.appendChild(select);
+    wrap.appendChild(label);
+
+    var atcap = make("span", "grant-atcap");
+    atcap.setAttribute("data-grant-atcap", "");
+    var groupName = "atcap-" + String((_atcapSeq += 1));
+    var inputs = [];
+    [["stop", "Stop"], ["propose", "Propose"]].forEach(function (pair) {
+      var radioLabel = make("label", null);
+      var input = make("input", null);
+      input.type = "radio";
+      input.name = groupName;
+      input.value = pair[0];
+      input.addEventListener("change", onChange);
+      inputs.push(input);
+      radioLabel.appendChild(input);
+      radioLabel.appendChild(make("span", null, pair[1]));
+      atcap.appendChild(radioLabel);
+    });
+    wrap.appendChild(atcap);
+
+    wrap.getGrant = function () {
+      if (select.value === "") {
+        return null;
+      }
+      var checked = null;
+      inputs.forEach(function (input) {
+        if (input.checked) {
+          checked = input;
+        }
+      });
+      if (checked === null) {
+        return null;
+      }
+      return { next_ceiling: select.value, at_cap: checked.value };
+    };
+    return wrap;
+  }
+
+  // --- D11 item 7: proposal card ---------------------------------------------
+  // edited_body is included iff the textarea differs (strict string) from the
+  // proposal body from this render's fetch (resolution.py:151-161). When
+  // requireGrant, Accept is unfireable until the picker yields both halves.
+  function proposalCard(opts) {
+    var card = make("div", "proposal-card");
+    card.appendChild(make("div", "proposal-meta", "proposed by " + opts.proposal.proposed_by));
+    card.appendChild(markdownBlock(opts.proposal.body));
+    var textarea = make("textarea", "field-editor-input proposal-edit");
+    textarea.setAttribute("data-edit", "");
+    textarea.rows = 8;
+    textarea.value = opts.proposal.body;
+    card.appendChild(textarea);
+
+    var picker = null;
+    var inFlight = false;
+    var resolved = false;
+    var actions = make("div", "proposal-card-actions");
+    var accept = make("button", "button button--primary", "Accept");
+    accept.type = "button";
+    accept.setAttribute("data-accept", "");
+
+    function sync() {
+      accept.disabled = inFlight || resolved ||
+        (opts.requireGrant && (picker === null || picker.getGrant() === null));
+    }
+
+    if (opts.requireGrant) {
+      picker = grantPairPicker(opts.newState, sync);
+      card.appendChild(picker);
+    }
+
+    accept.addEventListener("click", function () {
+      var prior = actions.querySelector(".error-line");
+      if (prior) {
+        actions.removeChild(prior);
+      }
+      var payload = {};
+      if (textarea.value !== opts.proposal.body) {
+        payload.edited_body = textarea.value;
+      }
+      if (opts.requireGrant) {
+        var grant = picker.getGrant();
+        payload.next_ceiling = grant.next_ceiling;
+        payload.at_cap = grant.at_cap;
+      }
+      inFlight = true;
+      sync();
+      Promise.resolve(opts.onAccept(payload)).then(
+        function () {
+          resolved = true;   // A3: stay disabled after a successful accept
+          inFlight = false;
+          sync();
+        },
+        function (err) {
+          inFlight = false;
+          actions.prepend(errorLine(err));
+          sync();
+        }
+      );
+    });
+
+    actions.appendChild(accept);
+    card.appendChild(actions);
+    sync();   // Accept starts disabled when a grant is required (nothing picked)
+    return card;
+  }
+
+  // --- D11 item 9: day-plan tree ---------------------------------------------
+  // node is "root" (string) or the child position (NUMBER — the server rejects
+  // string positions, days/api.py:83-91). No local status mutation: the four
+  // routes append events, so the WS flush re-render shows the new tree.
+  function planTree(plan, handlers) {
+    var wrap = make("div", "plan-tree");
+    var actions = make("div", "plan-tree-actions");
+
+    var acceptAll = make("button", "button button--primary", "Accept all");
+    acceptAll.type = "button";
+    acceptAll.setAttribute("data-accept-all", "");
+    bindMutating(acceptAll, actions, function () {
+      return handlers.acceptAll();
+    });
+    var rejectAll = make("button", "button", "Reject all");
+    rejectAll.type = "button";
+    rejectAll.setAttribute("data-reject-all", "");
+    bindMutating(rejectAll, actions, function () {
+      return handlers.rejectAll();
+    });
+    actions.appendChild(acceptAll);
+    actions.appendChild(rejectAll);
+    wrap.appendChild(actions);
+
+    function makeNode(ref, isRoot, label, status, ticketId) {
+      var node = make("div", isRoot ? "plan-node plan-node--root" : "plan-node plan-node--child");
+      node.setAttribute("data-node", isRoot ? "root" : String(ref));
+      node.setAttribute("data-status", status);
+      node.appendChild(make("span", "plan-node-status", status));
+      node.appendChild(make("span", isRoot ? "plan-node-focus" : "plan-node-note", label));
+      if (!isRoot && ticketId) {
+        var link = make("a", "plan-node-ticket", "open");
+        link.setAttribute("href", "#/ticket/" + ticketId);
+        node.appendChild(link);
+      }
+      var nodeActions = make("span", "plan-node-actions");
+      if (status !== "accepted") {   // Accept only offered on a not-yet-accepted node
+        var acceptNode = make("button", "button", "Accept");
+        acceptNode.type = "button";
+        acceptNode.setAttribute("data-accept", "");
+        bindMutating(acceptNode, actions, function () {
+          return handlers.accept(ref);
+        });
+        nodeActions.appendChild(acceptNode);
+      }
+      var invalidate = make("button", "button", "Invalidate");
+      invalidate.type = "button";
+      invalidate.setAttribute("data-invalidate", "");
+      bindMutating(invalidate, actions, function () {
+        return handlers.invalidate(ref);
+      });
+      nodeActions.appendChild(invalidate);
+      node.appendChild(nodeActions);
+      return node;
+    }
+
+    wrap.appendChild(makeNode("root", true, plan.root.focus, plan.root.status, null));
+    (plan.children || []).forEach(function (child) {
+      wrap.appendChild(
+        makeNode(child.position, false, child.note || child.ticket_id, child.status, child.ticket_id)
+      );
+    });
+    return wrap;
+  }
+
+  // --- D11 item 10: chat panel -----------------------------------------------
+  // entityId is the SERVER-provided chat id (day.id / ticket.id), never built
+  // client-side. Rebuilds its message list from the module-scope transcript, which
+  // the WS flush does not touch — that is the survival mechanism.
+  function chatPanel(entityId, opts) {
+    var panelEl = make("div", "chat-panel");
+    panelEl.setAttribute("data-chat-panel", "");
+    var messages = make("div", "chat-messages");
+    messages.setAttribute("data-chat-messages", "");
+    panelEl.appendChild(messages);
+
+    function paint() {
+      messages.replaceChildren();
+      (chatTranscripts[entityId] || []).forEach(function (msg) {
+        if (msg.who === "you") {
+          var you = make("div", "chat-msg chat-msg--you", msg.text);
+          you.setAttribute("data-chat-msg", "you");
+          messages.appendChild(you);
+        } else {
+          var reply = make("div", "chat-msg chat-msg--planner");
+          reply.setAttribute("data-chat-msg", "planner");
+          reply.appendChild(markdownBlock(msg.text));
+          messages.appendChild(reply);
+        }
+      });
+    }
+    paint();
+
+    if (!opts.available) {
+      var offline = make("div", "chat-offline", "gateway offline");
+      offline.setAttribute("data-chat-offline", "");
+      panelEl.appendChild(offline);
+      return panelEl;   // §11 notice; no input source rendered when offline
+    }
+
+    var source;
+    var ctx = {
+      initialText: chatDrafts[entityId] || "",
+      onInput: function (text) {
+        chatDrafts[entityId] = text;
+      },
+      submit: function (text) {
+        if (!chatTranscripts[entityId]) {
+          chatTranscripts[entityId] = [];
+        }
+        chatTranscripts[entityId].push({ who: "you", text: text });
+        delete chatDrafts[entityId];
+        paint();
+        return Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/chat/" + entityId + "/send", {
+          method: "POST",
+          body: { text: text }
+        }).then(
+          function (res) {
+            chatTranscripts[entityId].push({ who: "planner", text: res.reply_text });
+            if (panelEl.isConnected) {   // a flush may have replaced the panel mid-flight
+              paint();
+            }
+            return res;
+          },
+          function (err) {
+            var prior = panelEl.querySelector(".error-line");
+            if (prior) {
+              panelEl.removeChild(prior);
+            }
+            panelEl.insertBefore(errorLine(err), source);
+            return Promise.reject(err);
+          }
+        );
+      }
+    };
+    source = Planner.chatInput.sources[Planner.chatInput.active](ctx);
+    panelEl.appendChild(source);
+    return panelEl;
+  }
+
+  // --- D11 item 16: review card ----------------------------------------------
+  // The shell is panel() (D11 #2 — the only box primitive). The screen guards
+  // staleness before constructing (proposal null / state moved on).
+  function reviewCard(opts) {
+    var entry = opts.entry;
+    var detail = opts.detail;
+    var kind = entry.kind;
+    var gating = kind === "success" || kind === "approach" ||
+      kind === "plan" || kind === "result";
+    var content = [];
+    var head = make("div", "review-card-head");
+    var footer = make("div", "review-card-actions");
+    var primary = null;
+
+    if (gating) {
+      head.appendChild(chip("state", detail.state));
+      head.appendChild(chip("pending-proposal"));
+      content.push(head);
+      content.push(proposalCard({
+        proposal: detail.fields[kind].proposal,
+        requireGrant: true,
+        newState: advanceTarget(detail.state, detail.ceiling),
+        onAccept: opts.onAccept
+      }));
+    } else if (kind === "review") {
+      head.appendChild(chip("state", detail.state));
+      content.push(head);
+      content.push(markdownBlock(detail.fields.result.value));
+      if (detail.fields.result.notes) {
+        var note = make("div", "review-note");
+        note.appendChild(markdownBlock(detail.fields.result.notes));
+        content.push(note);
+      }
+      primary = make("button", "button button--primary", "Approve");
+      primary.type = "button";
+      primary.setAttribute("data-approve", "");
+      bindMutating(primary, footer, function () {
+        return opts.onApprove();
+      });
+    } else if (kind === "status") {
+      head.appendChild(chip(null, detail.status));
+      head.appendChild(chip(null, "→ " + detail.status_proposal.to_status));
+      content.push(head);
+      if (detail.status_proposal.note) {
+        content.push(make("div", "review-note", detail.status_proposal.note));
+      }
+      primary = make("button", "button button--primary", "Accept");
+      primary.type = "button";
+      primary.setAttribute("data-accept-status", "");
+      bindMutating(primary, footer, function () {
+        return opts.onAcceptStatus();
+      });
+    }
+
+    if (primary) {
+      footer.appendChild(primary);
+    }
+    var skip = make("button", "button", "Skip");
+    skip.type = "button";
+    skip.setAttribute("data-skip", "");
+    skip.addEventListener("click", function () {
+      opts.onSkip();
+    });
+    footer.appendChild(skip);
+    if (entry.entity_type === "ticket") {
+      var open = make("a", "review-open", "open ticket");
+      open.setAttribute("data-open-ticket", "");
+      open.setAttribute("href", "#/ticket/" + entry.entity_id);
+      footer.appendChild(open);
+    }
+    content.push(footer);
+
+    var section = panel(entry.title, content);
+    section.setAttribute("data-review-card", "");
+    section.setAttribute("data-entity-id", entry.entity_id);
+    section.setAttribute("data-kind", kind);
+    if (gating) {
+      section.setAttribute("data-field", kind);
+    }
+    return section;
+  }
+
+  // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
+  // T16 additions (D11 items 11, 12, 14, 15) — State control, Grant control,
+  // Event log, Run history. Additive: nothing above this line is touched. These
+  // reuse the T15 grant-pair picker and the shared bindMutating discipline; the
+  // server re-validates every write, so the pickers only choose what to OFFER.
+  // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+  function stateLabel(s) {
+    return String(s).replace(/_/g, " ");
+  }
+
+  function formatUnix(seconds) {
+    if (seconds === null || seconds === undefined) {
+      return "";
+    }
+    return new Date(Number(seconds) * 1000).toLocaleString();
+  }
+
+  // The exact event-row summary (payload keys confirmed against resolution.py /
+  // api.py writers). Unlisted kinds get an empty summary; kind + time still show.
+  function eventSummary(kind, payload) {
+    payload = payload || {};
+    if (kind === "state_changed") {
+      return String(payload.from) + " → " + String(payload.to);
+    }
+    if (kind === "proposal_accepted") {
+      return String(payload.field) + " · " + String(payload.resolved_by);
+    }
+    if (kind === "ticket_updated") {
+      return String(payload.field);
+    }
+    return "";
+  }
+
+  // D11 #11: state control — human jump / drop / (conditional) unblock. No
+  // re-render on success; the WS flush handles it (bindMutating stays disabled).
+  function stateControl(opts) {
+    var wrap = make("div", "state-control");
+    wrap.setAttribute("data-state-control", "");
+    wrap.appendChild(make("span", "state-control-current", stateLabel(opts.state)));
+
+    var select = make("select", "state-control-select form-control");
+    select.setAttribute("data-state-select", "");
+    STATE_ORDER.forEach(function (s) {
+      var option = make("option", null, stateLabel(s));
+      option.value = s;
+      select.appendChild(option);
+    });
+    select.value = opts.state;
+    wrap.appendChild(select);
+
+    var errorHost = make("div", "state-control-error");
+
+    var jump = make("button", "button", "Jump");
+    jump.type = "button";
+    jump.setAttribute("data-state-jump", "");
+    bindMutating(jump, errorHost, function () {
+      return opts.onJump(select.value);
+    });
+    wrap.appendChild(jump);
+
+    var drop = make("button", "button", "Drop");
+    drop.type = "button";
+    drop.setAttribute("data-drop", "");
+    bindMutating(drop, errorHost, function () {
+      return opts.onDrop();
+    });
+    wrap.appendChild(drop);
+
+    if (opts.autoBlocked) {
+      var unblock = make("button", "button", "Unblock");
+      unblock.type = "button";
+      unblock.setAttribute("data-unblock", "");
+      bindMutating(unblock, errorHost, function () {
+        return opts.onUnblock();
+      });
+      wrap.appendChild(unblock);
+    }
+
+    wrap.appendChild(errorHost);
+    return wrap;
+  }
+
+  // D11 #12: grant control — plain ceiling/at-cap pickers via the T15 grant-pair
+  // picker (no dial). Save is disabled until BOTH picker halves are chosen. "No
+  // further" (next_ceiling === "none") means the ceiling becomes exactly the
+  // current state (resolve_grant, machine.py:87-89).
+  function grantControl(opts) {
+    var wrap = make("div", "grant-control");
+    wrap.setAttribute("data-grant-control", "");
+
+    // Raw values (not stateLabel'd) so the persisted grant is asserted verbatim.
+    var current = make(
+      "div", "grant-control-current",
+      "ceiling: " + opts.ceiling + " · at-cap: " + opts.atCap
+    );
+    current.setAttribute("data-grant-current", "");
+    wrap.appendChild(current);
+
+    var errorHost = make("div", "grant-control-error");
+
+    var save = make("button", "button button--primary", "Save");
+    save.type = "button";
+    save.setAttribute("data-grant-save", "");
+    save.disabled = true;
+
+    var picker = grantPairPicker(opts.state, function () {
+      save.disabled = picker.getGrant() === null;
+    });
+    wrap.appendChild(picker);
+
+    bindMutating(save, errorHost, function () {
+      var grant = picker.getGrant();
+      var ceiling = grant.next_ceiling === "none" ? opts.state : grant.next_ceiling;
+      return opts.onSave(ceiling, grant.at_cap);
+    });
+    wrap.appendChild(save);
+    wrap.appendChild(errorHost);
+    return wrap;
+  }
+
+  // D11 #14: event log — events as served (ascending id), rendered in order.
+  function eventLog(events) {
+    var wrap = make("div", "event-log");
+    wrap.setAttribute("data-event-log", "");
+    if (!events || !events.length) {
+      wrap.appendChild(quietLine("(no events)"));

+      wrap.appendChild(row);

+    var wrap = make("div", "run-history");
+    wrap.setAttribute("data-run-history", "");
+    if (!runs || !runs.length) {
+      wrap.appendChild(quietLine("(no runs)"));
+      return wrap;
+    }
+    runs.forEach(function (run) {
+      var row = make("div", "run-history-row");
+      row.setAttribute("data-run-row", "");
+      row.setAttribute("data-run-status", run.status);
+      row.appendChild(make("span", "run-history-status", run.status));
+      var times = formatUnix(run.started_at) +
+        (run.ended_at ? " – " + formatUnix(run.ended_at) : "");
+      row.appendChild(make("span", "run-history-times", times));
+      row.appendChild(make("span", "run-history-detail", run.summary || run.error || ""));
+      wrap.appendChild(row);
+    });
+    return wrap;
+  }

     1	/* Screen 4: Ticket (#/ticket/<id>) — T16 (SPEC §10.4). Stateless render over five
     2	 * parallel fetches (detail + events + runs + sprints + chat status). Field
     3	 * sections show the value as markdown; a pending proposal composes T15's

     6	 * Run history, Event log, and the T15 chat panel round it out. Every mutation calls
     7	 * fetchJson and relies on the WS flush -> route() re-render; no optimistic UI. The
     8	 * lone direct DOM tweak is the copy button's "Copied" flash (a read, not a
     9	 * WS-mutating action, so no flush follows). Classic script: IIFE + "use strict",
    10	 * createElement only. */
    11	(function () {
    12	  "use strict";
    13	  var Planner = window.Planner;
    14	  var config = Planner.config;
    15	  var C = Planner.components;
    16	  var api = Planner.api;
    17	
    18	  var LINK_KINDS = ["belongs_to", "parent_child", "blocks", "relates"];
    19	  var FIELD_NAMES = ["success", "approach", "plan", "result"];
    20	  var COPY_FLASH_MS = 1500;
    21	
    22	  function el(tag, className, text) {
    23	    var node = document.createElement(tag);
    24	    if (className) {
    25	      node.className = className;
    26	    }
    27	    if (text !== undefined && text !== null) {
    28	      node.textContent = text;
    29	    }
    30	    return node;
    31	  }
    32	
    33	  // Marker chip: value === variant, so "blocked" (no known chip variant) shows its
    34	  // own text via the default branch while known markers render their canned label.
    35	  function marker(v) {
    36	    var chip = C.chip(v, v);
    37	    chip.setAttribute("data-marker", v);
    38	    return chip;
    39	  }
    40	
    41	  // Plain-text fetch for copy-text: fetchJson would JSON.parse the text body and
    42	  // reject. The reject shape only needs .code/.message for errorLine.
    43	  function fetchText(path) {
    44	    return fetch(path).then(function (r) {
    45	      if (!r.ok) {
    46	        var err = new Error("HTTP " + r.status);
    47	        err.code = "http_error";
    48	        return Promise.reject(err);
    49	      }
    50	      return r.text();
    51	    });
    52	  }
    53	
    54	  function copyToClipboard(text) {
    55	    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
    56	      return navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    57	    }
    58	    return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
    59	      var ta = el("textarea");
    60	      ta.value = text;
    61	      ta.setAttribute("readonly", "");
    62	      ta.style.position = "fixed";
    63	      ta.style.opacity = "0";
    64	      document.body.appendChild(ta);
    65	      ta.select();
    66	      var copied = false;
    67	      try {
    68	        copied = document.execCommand("copy");
    69	      } catch (e) {
    70	        copied = false;
    71	      }
    72	      document.body.removeChild(ta);
    73	      if (copied) {
    74	        resolve();
    75	      } else {
    76	        reject(new Error("copy failed"));
    77	      }
    78	    });
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  // In-flight discipline for the screen's own buttons (links/day/sprint/copy): clear
    82	  // any prior error, disable, run. On rejection re-enable and surface the error;
    83	  // returns the promise so copy can flash after a successful clipboard write. For a
    84	  // WS-mutating action the caller does NOT re-enable — the flush re-render replaces
    85	  // the DOM.
    86	  function submit(button, errorHost, fn) {
    87	    var prior = errorHost.querySelector(".error-line");
    88	    if (prior) {
    89	      errorHost.removeChild(prior);
    90	    }
    91	    button.disabled = true;
    92	    return Promise.resolve(fn()).then(
    93	      function (res) {
    94	        return res;
    95	      },
    96	      function (err) {
    97	        button.disabled = false;
    98	        errorHost.prepend(C.errorLine(err));
    99	        return Promise.reject(err);
   100	      }
   101	    );
   102	  }
   103	
   104	  function selectEl(options, selectedValue) {
   105	    var select = el("select", "form-control");
   106	    options.forEach(function (opt) {
   107	      var option = el("option", null, opt.label);
   108	      option.value = opt.value;
   109	      select.appendChild(option);
   110	    });
   111	    if (selectedValue !== undefined && selectedValue !== null) {
   112	      select.value = selectedValue;
   113	    }
   114	    return select;
   115	  }

   697	  function reviewCard(opts) {
   698	    var entry = opts.entry;
   699	    var detail = opts.detail;
   700	    var kind = entry.kind;

   702	      kind === "plan" || kind === "result";

   714	        requireGrant: true,
   715	        newState: advanceTarget(detail.state, detail.ceiling),
   716	        onAccept: opts.onAccept
   717	      }));

   722	      if (detail.fields.result.notes) {

   730	      bindMutating(primary, footer, function () {
   731	        return opts.onApprove();
   732	      });
   733	    } else if (kind === "status") {
   734	      head.appendChild(chip(null, detail.status));
   735	      head.appendChild(chip(null, "→ " + detail.status_proposal.to_status));
   736	      content.push(head);

   738	        content.push(make("div", "review-note", detail.status_proposal.note));
   739	      }
   740	      primary = make("button", "button button--primary", "Accept");
   741	      primary.type = "button";
   742	      primary.setAttribute("data-accept-status", "");
   743	      bindMutating(primary, footer, function () {
   744	        return opts.onAcceptStatus();
   745	      });
   746	    }
   747

   776	  // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   777	  // T16 additions (D11 items 11, 12, 14, 15) — State control, Grant control,
   778	  // Event log, Run history. Additive: nothing above this line is touched. These
   779	  // reuse the T15 grant-pair picker and the shared bindMutating discipline; the
   780	  // server re-validates every write, so the pickers only choose what to OFFER.
   781	  // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   782	
   783	  function stateLabel(s) {
   784	    return String(s).replace(/_/g, " ");
   785	  }
   786	
   787	  function formatUnix(seconds) {
   788	    if (seconds === null || seconds === undefined) {
   789	      return "";
   790	    }
   791	    return new Date(Number(seconds) * 1000).toLocaleString();
   792	  }
   793	
   794	  // The exact event-row summary (payload keys confirmed against resolution.py /
   795	  // api.py writers). Unlisted kinds get an empty summary; kind + time still show.
   796	  function eventSummary(kind, payload) {
   797	    payload = payload || {};
   798	    if (kind === "state_changed") {
   799	      return String(payload.from) + " → " + String(payload.to);
   800	    }
   801	    if (kind === "proposal_accepted") {
   802	      return String(payload.field) + " · " + String(payload.resolved_by);
   803	    }
   804	    if (kind === "ticket_updated") {
   805	      return String(payload.field);
   806	    }
   807	    return "";
   808	  }
   809	
   810	  // D11 #11: state control — human jump / drop / (conditional) unblock. No
   811	  // re-render on success; the WS flush handles it (bindMutating stays disabled).
   812	  function stateControl(opts) {
   813	    var wrap = make("div", "state-control");
   814	    wrap.setAttribute("data-state-control", "");
   815	    wrap.appendChild(make("span", "state-control-current", stateLabel(opts.state)));
   816	
   817	    var select = make("select", "state-control-select form-control");
   818	    select.setAttribute("data-state-select", "");
   819	    STATE_ORDER.forEach(function (s) {
   820	      var option = make("option", null, stateLabel(s));
   821	      option.value = s;
   822	      select.appendChild(option);
   823	    });
   824	    select.value = opts.state;
   825	    wrap.appendChild(select);
   826	
   827	    var errorHost = make("div", "state-control-error");
   828	
   829	    var jump = make("button", "button", "Jump");
   830	    jump.type = "button";
   831	    jump.setAttribute("data-state-jump", "");
   832	    bindMutating(jump, errorHost, function () {
   833	      return opts.onJump(select.value);
   834	    });
   835	    wrap.appendChild(jump);
   836	
   837	    var drop = make("button", "button", "Drop");
   838	    drop.type = "button";
   839	    drop.setAttribute("data-drop", "");
   840	    bindMutating(drop, errorHost, function () {
   841	      return opts.onDrop();
   842	    });
   843	    wrap.appendChild(drop);
   844	
   845	    if (opts.autoBlocked) {
   846	      var unblock = make("button", "button", "Unblock");
   847	      unblock.type = "button";
   848	      unblock.setAttribute("data-unblock", "");
   849	      bindMutating(unblock, errorHost, function () {
   850	        return opts.onUnblock();
   851	      });
   852	      wrap.appendChild(unblock);
   853	    }
   854	
   855	    wrap.appendChild(errorHost);
   856	    return wrap;
   857	  }
   858	
   859	  // D11 #12: grant control — plain ceiling/at-cap pickers via the T15 grant-pair
   860	  // picker (no dial). Save is disabled until BOTH picker halves are chosen. "No
   861	  // further" (next_ceiling === "none") means the ceiling becomes exactly the
   862	  // current state (resolve_grant, machine.py:87-89).
   863	  function grantControl(opts) {
   864	    var wrap = make("div", "grant-control");
   865	    wrap.setAttribute("data-grant-control", "");
   866	
   867	    // Raw values (not stateLabel'd) so the persisted grant is asserted verbatim.
   868	    var current = make(
   869	      "div", "grant-control-current",
   870	      "ceiling: " + opts.ceiling + " · at-cap: " + opts.atCap
   871	    );
   872	    current.setAttribute("data-grant-current", "");
   873	    wrap.appendChild(current);
   874	
   875	    var errorHost = make("div", "grant-control-error");
   876	
   877	    var save = make("button", "button button--primary", "Save");
   878	    save.type = "button";
   879	    save.setAttribute("data-grant-save", "");
   880	    save.disabled = true;
   881	
   882	    var picker = grantPairPicker(opts.state, function () {
   883	      save.disabled = picker.getGrant() === null;
   884	    });
   885	    wrap.appendChild(picker);
   886	
   887	    bindMutating(save, errorHost, function () {
   888	      var grant = picker.getGrant();
   889	      var ceiling = grant.next_ceiling === "none" ? opts.state : grant.next_ceiling;
   890	      return opts.onSave(ceiling, grant.at_cap);
   891	    });
   892	    wrap.appendChild(save);
   893	    wrap.appendChild(errorHost);
   894	    return wrap;
   895	  }
   896	
   897	  // D11 #14: event log — events as served (ascending id), rendered in order.
   898	  function eventLog(events) {
   899	    var wrap = make("div", "event-log");
   900	    wrap.setAttribute("data-event-log", "");
   901	    if (!events || !events.length) {
   902	      wrap.appendChild(quietLine("(no events)"));
   903	      return wrap;
   904	    }
   905	    events.forEach(function (ev) {
   906	      var row = make("div", "event-log-row");
   907	      row.setAttribute("data-event-row", "");
   908	      row.setAttribute("data-event-kind", ev.kind);
   909	      row.appendChild(make("span", "event-log-kind", ev.kind));
   910	      row.appendChild(make("span", "event-log-summary", eventSummary(ev.kind, ev.payload)));
   911	      row.appendChild(make("span", "event-log-time", formatUnix(ev.created_at)));
   912	      wrap.appendChild(row);
   913	    });
   914	    return wrap;
   915	  }
   916	
   917	  // D11 #15: run history — runs as served (newest first), rendered in order.
   918	  function runHistory(runs) {
   919	    var wrap = make("div", "run-history");
   920	    wrap.setAttribute("data-run-history", "");
   921	    if (!runs || !runs.length) {
   922	      wrap.appendChild(quietLine("(no runs)"));
   923	      return wrap;
   924	    }
   925	    runs.forEach(function (run) {
   926	      var row = make("div", "run-history-row");
   927	      row.setAttribute("data-run-row", "");
   928	      row.setAttribute("data-run-status", run.status);
   929	      row.appendChild(make("span", "run-history-status", run.status));
   930	      var times = formatUnix(run.started_at) +
   931	        (run.ended_at ? " – " + formatUnix(run.ended_at) : "");
   932	      row.appendChild(make("span", "run-history-times", times));
   933	      row.appendChild(make("span", "run-history-detail", run.summary || run.error || ""));
   934	      wrap.appendChild(row);
   935	    });
   936	    return wrap;
   937	  }

   963	  // The pluggable chat-input registry (SPEC §14 audio seam). A later audio script
   964	  // registers a second source with one <script> tag and no edits here: chatPanel
   965	  // reads sources[active] at render time and couples only to the ctx contract.
   966	  Planner.chatInput = {
   967	    sources: { text: makeTextInputSource },
   968	    active: "text",

   970	      Planner.chatInput.sources[name] = factory;
   971	      if (makeActive) {
   972	        Planner.chatInput.active = name;
   973	      }
   974	    }

     1	"""Ticket routes (§9), plus the ticket-anchored links and the ticket-centric
     2	derived views (board, queues). Thin HTTP shells over the stage-3 writers and the
     3	pure read views: every handler is parse -> auth -> writer -> serialize. No route
     4	re-implements a domain rule and no route appends events.
     5	
     6	This module also homes the shared request plumbing (config/clock accessors, the
     7	per-request connection dependency, the transaction context manager, and the enum
     8	marshaller). The other api modules import these from here; the direction is strictly
     9	one-way (sprints/days/dispatch api -> tickets api), and views modules import no api
    10	module at all."""
    11	
    12	from __future__ import annotations
    13	
    14	import sqlite3
    15	from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Callable, Iterator
    16	from contextlib import contextmanager
    17	from enum import StrEnum
    18	from typing import Annotated, Any, cast

    56	
    57	def get_config(request: Request) -> Config:
    58	    return cast(Config, request.app.state.config)
    59	
    60	
    61	def get_clock(request: Request) -> Clock:
    62	    return cast(Clock, request.app.state.clock)
    63	
    64	
    65	async def db_conn(request: Request) -> AsyncIterator[sqlite3.Connection]:
    66	    conn = cast(Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection], request.app.state.conn_factory)()
    67	    try:
    68	        yield conn
    69	    finally:
    70	        conn.close()
    71	
    72	
    73	DbConn = Annotated[sqlite3.Connection, Depends(db_conn)]
    74	Ctx = Annotated[RequestContext, Depends(request_context)]
    75	Cfg = Annotated[Config, Depends(get_config)]
    76	Clk = Annotated[Clock, Depends(get_clock)]
    77	
    78	
    79	@contextmanager
    80	def txn(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> Iterator[None]:
    81	    """Wrap a NON-self-transacting writer (days/dispatch/links) so a mid-sequence
    82	    PlannerError rolls back cleanly. Never wrap a ticket/sprint writer — those open
    83	    their own BEGIN IMMEDIATE and would raise 'transaction within a transaction'."""
    84	    conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    85	    try:
    86	        yield
    87	    except BaseException:
    88	        conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
    89	        raise
    90	    else:
    91	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
    92	
    93	
    94	def parse_enum[E: StrEnum](enum_cls: type[E], raw: str, what: str) -> E:
    95	    try:
    96	        return enum_cls(raw)
    97	    except ValueError:
    98	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid {what}", {what: raw}) from None
    99

   146	# --- grant marshallers ---------------------------------------------------------
   147	
   148	
   149	def _parse_next_ceiling(raw: str | None) -> NextCeiling | None:
   150	    if raw is None:
   151	        return None
   152	    if raw == NO_FURTHER:
   153	        return NO_FURTHER
   154	    try:
   155	        return TicketState(raw)
   156	    except ValueError:
   157	        raise PlannerError(
   158	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown next_ceiling", {"next_ceiling": raw}
   159	        ) from None
   160	
   161	
   162	def _parse_grant_at_cap(raw: str | None) -> AtCap | None:
   163	    if raw is None:
   164	        return None
   165	    try:
   166	        return AtCap(raw)
   167	    except ValueError:
   168	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown at_cap", {"at_cap": raw}) from None
   169	
   170	
   171	# --- private gap-fill writers (D2; A1: writer-shaped for relocation) ------------
   172	
   173	
   174	def _set_title(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str, title: str, *,
   175	               title_max_chars: int, now: int) -> Ticket:
   176	    admission.validate_title(title, title_max_chars)
   177	    with txn(conn):
   178	        ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   179	        prev = ticket.title
   180	        conn.execute(
   181	            "UPDATE tickets SET title = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (title, now, ticket_id)
   182	        )
   183	        append_event(
   184	            conn,
   185	            ticket_id,
   186	            EventKind.ticket_updated,
   187	            {"field": "title", "from": prev, "to": title},
   188	            now,
   189	        )
   190	        return tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   191	
   192	
   193	def _set_project(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str, project: Project | None, *,
   194	                 now: int) -> Ticket:
   195	    with txn(conn):
   196	        ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   197	        if ticket.sprint_item_id is not None:
   198	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "project is derived when parented")
   199	        prev = ticket.project.value if ticket.project is not None else None
   200	        new_value = project.value if project is not None else None
   201	        conn.execute(
   202	            "UPDATE tickets SET project = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   203	            (new_value, now, ticket_id),
   204	        )
   205	        append_event(
   206	            conn,
   207	            ticket_id,
   208	            EventKind.ticket_updated,
   209	            {"field": "project", "from": prev, "to": new_value},
   210	            now,
   211	        )
   212	        return tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)

   218	@router.post("/tickets")
   219	async def create_ticket(body: CreateTicketBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg,
   220	                        clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   221	    now = clk.now_unix()
   222	    priority = parse_enum(Priority, body.priority, "priority") if body.priority is not None \
   223	        else Priority.P3
   224	    project = parse_enum(Project, body.project, "project") if body.project is not None else None
   225	    ticket = tickets_data.create_ticket(
   226	        conn,
   227	        title=body.title,
   228	        actor=ctx.actor,
   229	        now=now,
   230	        title_max_chars=cfg.title_max_chars,
   231	        project=project,
   232	        priority=priority,
   233	        deadline=body.deadline,
   234	        sprint_id=body.sprint_id,
   235	        sprint_item_id=body.sprint_item_id,
   236	    )
   237	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(ticket, now)

   262	
   263	@router.patch("/tickets/{ticket_id}")
   264	async def patch_ticket(ticket_id: str, body: dict[str, Any], conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   265	                       cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   266	    recognized = ("title", "priority", "deadline", "project", "sprint_id")
   267	    for key in body:
   268	        if key not in recognized:
   269	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "unknown ticket field", {"field": key})
   270	    if not body:
   271	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no ticket fields to update", {})
   272	    now = clk.now_unix()
   273	    if "title" in body:
   274	        _set_title(conn, ticket_id, body["title"], title_max_chars=cfg.title_max_chars, now=now)
   275	    if "priority" in body:
   276	        priority = parse_enum(Priority, body["priority"], "priority")
   277	        tickets_data.set_priority(conn, ticket_id, priority=priority, actor=ctx.actor, now=now)
   278	    if "deadline" in body:
   279	        tickets_data.set_deadline(conn, ticket_id, deadline=body["deadline"], actor=ctx.actor,
   280	                                  now=now)
   281	    if "project" in body:
   282	        project = parse_enum(Project, body["project"], "project") \
   283	            if body["project"] is not None else None
   284	        _set_project(conn, ticket_id, project, now=now)
   285	    if "sprint_id" in body:
   286	        tickets_data.set_sprint(conn, ticket_id, sprint_id=body["sprint_id"], actor=ctx.actor,
   287	                                now=now)
   288	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id), now)
   289	
   290	
   291	@router.post("/tickets/{ticket_id}/propose/{field}")
   292	async def propose_field(ticket_id: str, field: str, body: ProposeBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   293	                        clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   294	    field_enum = parse_enum(FieldName, field, "field")
   295	    now = clk.now_unix()
   296	    if ctx.is_claimed_agent:
   297	        require_claim(conn, ctx, ticket_id, now)
   298	    ticket = tickets_data.file_proposal(
   299	        conn, ticket_id, field=field_enum, body=body.body, actor=ctx.actor, now=now
   300	    )

   356	@router.post("/tickets/{ticket_id}/grant")
   357	async def grant_ticket(ticket_id: str, body: GrantBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   358	                       clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   359	    reject_agents(ctx)
   360	    now = clk.now_unix()
   361	    if body.ceiling is None or body.at_cap is None:
   362	        missing = [name for name, raw in (("ceiling", body.ceiling), ("at_cap", body.at_cap))
   363	                   if raw is None]
   364	        raise PlannerError(
   365	            ErrorCode.grant_missing, "grant requires ceiling and at_cap", {"missing": missing}
   366	        )
   367	    try:
   368	        ceiling = TicketState(body.ceiling)
   369	    except ValueError:
   370	        raise PlannerError(
   371	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown ceiling", {"ceiling": body.ceiling}
   372	        ) from None
   373	    try:
   374	        at_cap = AtCap(body.at_cap)
   375	    except ValueError:
   376	        raise PlannerError(
   377	            ErrorCode.grant_invalid, "unknown at_cap", {"at_cap": body.at_cap}
   378	        ) from None
   379	    ticket = tickets_data.change_grant(
   380	        conn, ticket_id, ceiling=ceiling, at_cap=at_cap, actor=ctx.actor, now=now
   381	    )
   382	    return tickets_views.ticket_json(ticket, now)

   458	    return tickets_views.queues_view(conn, now, today_iso, item_approval_rows, item_overdue_rows)

    49	# --- shared day helpers --------------------------------------------------------
    50	
    51	
    52	def resolve_day_id(date_seg: str, clock: Clock, config: Config) -> str:
    53	    """Amendment 10: `today` resolves through the planning date. A6: the ISO branch
    54	    parses then re-formats via ids.day_id, so compact forms ('20260704') still yield
    55	    canonical day_2026-07-04 ids — the raw segment is never string-formatted."""
    56	    if date_seg == "today":
    57	        return ids.day_id(planning_date(clock.now(), config.boundary_hour))
    58	    try:
    59	        parsed = date.fromisoformat(date_seg)
    60	    except ValueError:
    61	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid date", {"date": date_seg}) from None
    62	    return ids.day_id(parsed)
    63	
    64	
    65	def _day_view(conn: sqlite3.Connection, did: str, now: int) -> JsonDict:
    66	    # A2: read_day materializes an absent day (INSERT + event, multi-statement) on an
    67	    # autocommit connection, so this one read is wrapped; the rest run bare.
    68	    with txn(conn):
    69	        day = days_data.read_day(conn, did, now)
    70	    dts = days_data.list_day_tickets(conn, did)
    71	    return {
    72	        "id": day.id,
    73	        "brief": day.brief,
    74	        "notes": day.notes,
    75	        "plan": plan_tree.tree_to_dict(day.plan) if day.plan is not None else None,
    76	        "chat_session_key": day.chat_session_key,
    77	        "created_at": day.created_at,
    78	        "updated_at": day.updated_at,
    79	        "tickets": [ticket_json(read_ticket(conn, dt.ticket_id), now) for dt in dts],
    80	    }
    81	
    82	
    83	def _parse_node(raw: object) -> str | int:
    84	    if raw == "root":
    85	        return "root"
    86	    if isinstance(raw, int) and not isinstance(raw, bool):
    87	        return raw
    88	    raise PlannerError(
    89	        ErrorCode.validation, "node must be 'root' or a child position", {"node": raw}
    90	    )
    91	
    92	
    93	def _load_plan_or_error(conn: sqlite3.Connection, did: str) -> PlanTree:
    94	    plan = days_data.load_plan(conn, did)
    95	    if plan is None:
    96	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "day has no plan", {"day_id": did})
    97	    return plan
    98	
    99	
   100	def _require_child(tree: PlanTree, position: int) -> None:
   101	    if not any(child.position == position for child in tree.children):
   102	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no such plan node", {"node": position})
   103	
   104	
   105	# --- day routes ----------------------------------------------------------------
   106	
   107	
   108	@router.get("/day/{date}")
   109	async def get_day(date: str, conn: DbConn, cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   110	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   111	    return _day_view(conn, did, clk.now_unix())
   112	
   113	
   114	@router.patch("/day/{date}")
   115	async def patch_day(date: str, body: DayPatchBody, conn: DbConn, cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   116	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   117	    now = clk.now_unix()
   118	    if body.brief is None and body.notes is None:
   119	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no day fields to update", {})
   120	    if body.brief is not None:
   121	        with txn(conn):
   122	            days_data.set_brief(conn, did, body.brief, now)
   123	    if body.notes is not None:
   124	        with txn(conn):
   125	            days_data.set_notes(conn, did, body.notes, now)
   126	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)
   127	
   128	
   129	@router.post("/day/{date}/tickets")
   130	async def add_day_ticket(date: str, body: AddDayTicketBody, conn: DbConn, cfg: Cfg,
   131	                         clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   132	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   133	    now = clk.now_unix()
   134	    read_ticket(conn, body.ticket_id)  # existence guard (avoids a raw FK 500)
   135	    with txn(conn):
   136	        days_data.add_day_ticket(conn, did, body.ticket_id, now)
   137	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)
   138	
   139	
   140	@router.delete("/day/{date}/tickets/{ticket_id}")
   141	async def remove_day_ticket(date: str, ticket_id: str, conn: DbConn, cfg: Cfg,
   142	                            clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   143	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   144	    now = clk.now_unix()
   145	    with txn(conn):
   146	        days_data.remove_day_ticket(conn, did, ticket_id, now)
   147	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)
   148	
   149	
   150	@router.post("/day/{date}/plan/accept")
   151	async def plan_accept(date: str, body: PlanNodeBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg,
   152	                      clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   153	    reject_agents(ctx)
   154	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   155	    now = clk.now_unix()
   156	    node = _parse_node(body.node)
   157	    tree = _load_plan_or_error(conn, did)
   158	    if isinstance(node, int):
   159	        _require_child(tree, node)
   160	    new_tree, effects = plan_tree.accept_node(tree, node)
   161	    with txn(conn):
   162	        days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)
   163	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)
   164	
   165	
   166	@router.post("/day/{date}/plan/accept-all")
   167	async def plan_accept_all(date: str, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   168	    reject_agents(ctx)
   169	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   170	    now = clk.now_unix()
   171	    tree = _load_plan_or_error(conn, did)
   172	    new_tree, effects = plan_tree.accept_all(tree)
   173	    with txn(conn):
   174	        days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)
   175	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)
   176	
   177	
   178	@router.post("/day/{date}/plan/invalidate")
   179	async def plan_invalidate(date: str, body: PlanNodeBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg,
   180	                          clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
     1	"""Chat routes (§9): send a message, read gateway availability."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import sqlite3
     6	from collections.abc import Callable
     7	from dataclasses import asdict

     9

    12	from planner.chat import service

    16	
    17	router = APIRouter()
    18	
    19	
    20	@router.post("/chat/{entity_id}/send")
    21	async def send_message(
    22	    entity_id: str, body: dict[str, Any], request: Request
    23	) -> dict[str, Any]:
    24	    text = body.get("text")
    25	    if not isinstance(text, str):
    26	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "text is required")
    27	    clock: Clock = request.app.state.clock
    28	    adapters: Adapters = request.app.state.adapters
    29	    conn_factory: Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection] = request.app.state.conn_factory
    30	    conn = conn_factory()
    31	    try:
    32	        result = service.send(conn, adapters.gateway, entity_id, text, clock.now_unix())
    33	    finally:
    34	        conn.close()
    35	    return asdict(result)
    36	
    37	
    38	@router.get("/chat/{entity_id}/status")
    39	async def gateway_status(entity_id: str, request: Request) -> dict[str, Any]:
    40	    # Availability is gateway-wide; {entity_id} keeps the per-entity route shape.
    41	    adapters: Adapters = request.app.state.adapters
    42	    result = service.status(adapters.gateway)
    43	    return {"available": result.available}

     1	/* Data layer: JSON fetch wrapper (structured-error passthrough), the WS client,
     2	 * and the invalidation bus (SPEC §9). No client-side state store — the bus holds
     3	 * only a cursor, a debounce timer, a backoff delay, and one flush callback; every
     4	 * flush makes the active screen refetch. WS batches are an invalidation signal:
     5	 * onmessage reads only msg.cursor, never the events array.
     6	 * Classic script: attaches Planner.api and Planner.bus; defines window.__plannerDebug. */
     7	(function () {
     8	  "use strict";
     9	  var Planner = (window.Planner = window.Planner || {});
    10	  var config = Planner.config;
    11	
    12	  // The sanctioned console hook: counters only, one console.debug per flush / open.
    13	  window.__plannerDebug = { flushes: 0, wsOpens: 0, cursor: 0 };

    15	  function makeError(code, message, extra) {
    16	    var err = new Error(message);
    17	    err.code = code;
    18	    if (extra) {
    19	      Object.keys(extra).forEach(function (key) {
    20	        err[key] = extra[key];
    21	      });
    22	    }
    23	    return err;
    24	  }
    25	
    26	  function makePlannerError(envelope, status) {
    27	    var err = new Error(envelope.message);
    28	    err.code = envelope.code;
    29	    err.detail = envelope.detail;
    30	    err.status = status;
    31	    err.isPlannerError = true;
    32	    return err;
    33	  }
    34	
    35	  // --- fetchJson ---------------------------------------------------------------
    36	
    37	  function fetchJson(path, options) {
    38	    options = options || {};
    39	    var init = { method: options.method || "GET", headers: {} };
    40	    if (options.body !== undefined && options.body !== null) {
    41	      init.body = JSON.stringify(options.body);
    42	      init.headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";
    43	    }
    44	    return fetch(path, init).then(
    45	      function (response) {
    46	        return response.text().then(function (raw) {
    47	          return { response: response, raw: raw };
    48	        });
    49	      },
    50	      function () {
    51	        return Promise.reject(makeError("network", "network error"));
    52	      }
    53	    ).then(function (pair) {
    54	      var response = pair.response;
    55	      var raw = pair.raw;
    56	      if (response.ok) {
    57	        try {
    58	          return raw === "" ? {} : JSON.parse(raw);
    59	        } catch (parseErr) {
    60	          return Promise.reject(
    61	            makeError("bad_json", "invalid JSON in response", { status: response.status })
    62	          );
    63	        }
    64	      }
    65	      var parsed = null;
    66	      try {
    67	        parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    68	      } catch (parseErr) {
    69	        parsed = null;
    70	      }
    71	      if (parsed && parsed.error && parsed.error.code) {
    72	        return Promise.reject(makePlannerError(parsed.error, response.status));
    73	      }
    74	      return Promise.reject(
    75	        makeError("http_error", "HTTP " + response.status, { status: response.status })
    76	      );
    77	    });
    78	  }
    79	
    80	  Planner.api = { fetchJson: fetchJson };
    81	
    82	  // --- the invalidation bus ----------------------------------------------------
    83	
    84	  var cursor = 0;
    85	  var socket = null;
    86	  var debounceMs = config.DEBOUNCE_MS_DEFAULT;
    87	  var flushTimer = null;
    88	  var retryMs = config.WS_RETRY_MIN_MS;
    89	  var onFlush = null;
    90	  var started = false;
    91	
    92	  function flush() {
    93	    flushTimer = null;
    94	    window.__plannerDebug.flushes += 1;
    95	    console.debug("[planner] flush " + window.__plannerDebug.flushes);
    96	    if (onFlush) {
    97	      onFlush();
    98	    }
    99	  }
   100	
   101	  function schedule() {
   102	    clearTimeout(flushTimer);
   103	    flushTimer = setTimeout(flush, debounceMs);
   104	  }
   105	
   106	  function connect() {
   107	    var scheme = location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss://" : "ws://";
   108	    var url = scheme + location.host + config.API.EVENTS_WS + "?since=" + cursor;
   109	    socket = new WebSocket(url);
   110	    socket.onopen = function () {
   111	      retryMs = config.WS_RETRY_MIN_MS;
   112	      window.__plannerDebug.wsOpens += 1;
   113	      console.debug("[planner] ws open since=" + cursor);
   114	    };
   115	    socket.onmessage = function (event) {
   116	      var msg;
   117	      try {
   118	        msg = JSON.parse(event.data);
   119	      } catch (parseErr) {
   120	        console.debug("[planner] ws bad json");
   121	        return;
   122	      }
   123	      if (typeof msg.cursor === "number") {
   124	        cursor = msg.cursor;
   125	        window.__plannerDebug.cursor = cursor;
   126	      }
   127	      schedule();
   128	    };
   129	    socket.onclose = function () {
   130	      socket = null;
   131	      setTimeout(connect, retryMs);
   132	      retryMs = Math.min(retryMs * config.WS_RETRY_FACTOR, config.WS_RETRY_MAX_MS);
   133	    };
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  Planner.bus = {
   137	    onInvalidate: function (fn) {
   138	      onFlush = fn;
   139	    },
   140	    start: function (ms) {
   141	      if (started) {
   142	        return;
   143	      }
   144	      started = true;
   145	      debounceMs = ms;
   146	      connect();
   147	    }
   148	  };

     1	/* Hash router, screen registry, and boot. Owns the shell instance. The router
     2	 * exposes exactly Planner.registerScreen(name, render) — screens navigate via

    34	  function route() {
    35	    var hash = location.hash;
    36	    if (hash === "" || hash === "#") {
    37	      location.replace(config.ROUTES.day);
    38	      return;
    39	    }

    41	    var name = segments[0];
    42	    var params = {};
    43	    if (name === "ticket") {
    44	      params.id = segments[1];
    45	    }
    46	    var known = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(registry, name);
    47	    // Exact route shapes only: #/<screen> or #/ticket/<id> — no trailing extras.
    48	    var badShape = name === "ticket"
    49	      ? segments.length !== 2 || !params.id
    50	      : segments.length !== 1;

    52	      shell.setActiveNav(null);
    53	      shell.content.replaceChildren(quietLine("no such screen"));
    54	      return;
    55	    }
    56	    shell.setActiveNav(name);
    57	    var entrance = hash !== lastHash;
    58	    lastHash = hash;
    59	    var screenDiv = document.createElement("div");
    60	    screenDiv.className = entrance ? "screen screen-enter" : "screen";
    61	    shell.content.replaceChildren(screenDiv);
    62	    registry[name].render(screenDiv, params);
    63	  }

    72	      }
    73	    );
    74	  }
    75

     1	/* Minimal safe markdown renderer. The ONLY innerHTML assignment in the whole
     2	 * codebase lives here — and it receives a string whose every character of user
     3	 * origin was HTML-escaped BEFORE any parsing, so no un-entified "<", ">", or quote
     4	 * can exist. Every tag is renderer-authored from a closed set; the only attribute
     5	 * emitted is a scheme-whitelisted href. Classic script: attaches Planner.markdown. */
     6	(function () {
     7	  "use strict";
     8	  var Planner = (window.Planner = window.Planner || {});
     9	
    10	  // Code-span placeholder sentinel (NUL). Stripped from raw input first, so a
    11	  // user string can never forge a placeholder.
    12	  var NUL = String.fromCharCode(0);
    13	  var RESTORE_RE = new RegExp(NUL + "(\\d+)" + NUL, "g");
    14	
    15	  function escapeHtml(s) {
    16	    return s
    17	      .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    18	      .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    19	      .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    20	      .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    21	      .replace(/'/g, "&#39;");
    22	  }
    23	
    24	  var SCHEME_RE = /^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/;
    25	
    26	  function safeHref(url) {
    27	    // Validate a normalized copy: browsers strip leading C0 controls/spaces
    28	    // (and interior tab/newline) when parsing URLs, so "\u0001javascript:..."
    29	    // would read as scheme-less here yet execute after normalization. Our strip
    30	    // is a superset of the browser's, so anything passing this check cannot
    31	    // normalize to a non-whitelisted scheme.
    32	    var norm = url.replace(/[\u0000-\u0020]+/g, "").toLowerCase();
    33	    if (SCHEME_RE.test(norm)) {
    34	      return (
    35	        norm.indexOf("http:") === 0 ||
    36	        norm.indexOf("https:") === 0 ||
    37	        norm.indexOf("mailto:") === 0
    38	      );
    39	    }
    40	    return true; // scheme-less (relative, "#/ticket/...", "?", "/") is allowed
    41	  }
    42	
    43	  function renderInline(line) {
    44	    var codes = [];
    45	    // 1. Code spans first — protected from all further processing.
    46	    line = line.replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, function (match, inner) {
    47	      var index = codes.length;
    48	      codes.push(inner);
    49	      return NUL + index + NUL;
    50	    });
    51	    // 2. Links — href is scheme-whitelisted; the value is already escaped.
    52	    line = line.replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^()\s]+)\)/g, function (match, text, url) {
    53	      if (safeHref(url)) {
    54	        return '<a href="' + url + '">' + text + "</a>";
    55	      }
    56	      return match;
    57	    });
    58	    // 3. Bold before italic so "**" is not eaten by "*".
    59	    line = line.replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, "<strong>$1</strong>");
    60	    // 4. Italic.
    61	    line = line.replace(/\*([^*]+)\*/g, "<em>$1</em>");
    62	    line = line.replace(/_([^_]+)_/g, "<em>$1</em>");
    63	    // 5. Restore code spans.
    64	    line = line.replace(RESTORE_RE, function (match, index) {
    65	      return "<code>" + codes[Number(index)] + "</code>";
    66	    });
    67	    return line;
    68	  }
    69	
    70	  function render(text) {
    71	    // 1. Strip the placeholder sentinel so collisions are impossible.
    72	    text = String(text).split(NUL).join("");
    73	    // 2. Escape everything once, before any parsing.
    74	    var escaped = escapeHtml(text);
    75	    var lines = escaped.split("\n");
    76	    var out = [];
    77	    var para = null;
    78	    var listType = null;
    79	    var listItems = null;
    80	    var i = 0;
    81	
    82	    function closePara() {
    83	      if (para) {
    84	        out.push("<p>" + para.join("<br>") + "</p>");
    85	        para = null;
    86	      }
    87	    }
    88	    function closeList() {
    89	      if (listItems) {
    90	        out.push("<" + listType + ">" + listItems.join("") + "</" + listType + ">");
    91	        listItems = null;
    92	        listType = null;
    93	      }
    94	    }
    95	    function closeBlocks() {
    96	      closePara();
    97	      closeList();
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    while (i < lines.length) {
   101	      var line = lines[i];
   102	      // Fenced code block — verbatim, no inline processing (already escaped).
   103	      if (/^```/.test(line)) {
   104	        closeBlocks();
   105	        i += 1;
   106	        var codeLines = [];
   107	        while (i < lines.length && !/^```/.test(lines[i])) {
   108	          codeLines.push(lines[i]);
   109	          i += 1;
   110	        }
   111	        if (i < lines.length) {
   112	          i += 1; // consume the closing fence
   113	        }
   114	        out.push("<pre><code>" + codeLines.join("\n") + "</code></pre>");
   115	        continue;
   116	      }
   117	      // Blank line closes the open block.
   118	      if (/^\s*$/.test(line)) {
   119	        closeBlocks();
   120	        i += 1;
   121	        continue;
   122	      }
   123	      // Heading — caps at h3 (four or more "#" render as h3).
   124	      var heading = /^(#{1,6})\s+(.*)$/.exec(line);
   125	      if (heading) {
   126	        closeBlocks();
   127	        var level = heading[1].length;
   128	        if (level > 3) {
   129	          level = 3;
   130	        }
   131	        out.push("<h" + level + ">" + renderInline(heading[2]) + "</h" + level + ">");
   132	        i += 1;
   133	        continue;
   134	      }
   135	      // Unordered list item.
   136	      var unordered = /^[-*+]\s+(.*)$/.exec(line);
   137	      if (unordered) {
   138	        closePara();
   139	        if (listType && listType !== "ul") {
   140	          closeList();

    68	        "ticket_id": str(row["ticket_id"]),
    69	        "status": str(row["status"]),
    70	        "started_at": int(row["started_at"]),
    71	        "ended_at": int(row["ended_at"]) if row["ended_at"] is not None else None,
    72	        "summary": str(row["summary"]) if row["summary"] is not None else None,
    73	        "error": str(row["error"]) if row["error"] is not None else None,
    74	        "pid": int(row["pid"]) if row["pid"] is not None else None,
    75	    }

    77	
    78	def event_json(row: sqlite3.Row) -> JsonDict:
    79	    return {
    80	        "id": int(row["id"]),
    81	        "entity_id": str(row["entity_id"]),
    82	        "kind": str(row["kind"]),
    83	        "payload": json.loads(str(row["payload"])),
    84	        "created_at": int(row["created_at"]),

    88	# --- ticket reads --------------------------------------------------------------
    89	
    90	
    91	def list_tickets(
    92	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    93	    now: int,
    94	    *,
    95	    state: TicketState | None,
    96	    project: Project | None,
    97	    sprint_id: str | None,
    98	    sprint_item_id: str | None,
    99	) -> list[JsonDict]:
   100	    clauses: list[str] = []
   101	    params: list[str] = []
   102	    if state is not None:
   103	        clauses.append("state = ?")
   104	        params.append(state.value)
   105	    if project is not None:
   106	        clauses.append("project = ?")
   107	        params.append(project.value)
   108	    if sprint_id is not None:
   109	        clauses.append("sprint_id = ?")
   110	        params.append(sprint_id)
   111	    if sprint_item_id is not None:
   112	        clauses.append("sprint_item_id = ?")
   113	        params.append(sprint_item_id)
   114	    where = (" WHERE " + " AND ".join(clauses)) if clauses else ""
   115	    rows = conn.execute(
   116	        "SELECT id FROM tickets" + where + " ORDER BY created_at ASC, id", tuple(params)
   117	    ).fetchall()
   118	    return [ticket_json(tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, str(row["id"])), now) for row in rows]
   119	
   120	
   121	def ticket_detail(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str, now: int) -> JsonDict:
   122	    ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   123	    detail = ticket_json(ticket, now)
   124	    link_rows = conn.execute(
   125	        "SELECT from_id, to_id, kind FROM links WHERE from_id = ? OR to_id = ? "
   126	        "ORDER BY kind, from_id, to_id",
   127	        (ticket_id, ticket_id),
   128	    ).fetchall()
   129	    day_rows = conn.execute(
   130	        "SELECT day_id FROM day_tickets WHERE ticket_id = ? ORDER BY day_id ASC", (ticket_id,)
   131	    ).fetchall()
   132	    total = int(
   133	        conn.execute(
   134	            "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ?", (ticket_id,)
   135	        ).fetchone()["n"]
   136	    )
   137	    running = conn.execute(
   138	        "SELECT 1 FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ? AND status = 'running' LIMIT 1", (ticket_id,)
   139	    ).fetchone()
   140	    latest = conn.execute(
   141	        "SELECT * FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ? ORDER BY started_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1",
   142	        (ticket_id,),
   143	    ).fetchone()
   144	    detail.update(
   145	        {
   146	            "blocked": core_links.is_blocked(conn, ticket_id),
   147	            "effective_sprint_id": tickets_data.get_effective_sprint_id(conn, ticket_id),
   148	            "links": [
   149	                {"from_id": str(r["from_id"]), "to_id": str(r["to_id"]), "kind": str(r["kind"])}
   150	                for r in link_rows
   151	            ],
   152	            "day_ids": [str(r["day_id"]) for r in day_rows],
   153	            "run_summary": {
   154	                "total": total,
   155	                "running": running is not None,
   156	                "latest": run_json(latest) if latest is not None else None,
   157	            },
   158	        }
   159	    )
   160	    return detail
   161	
   162	
   163	def runs_for_ticket(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str) -> list[JsonDict]:
   164	    rows = conn.execute(
   165	        "SELECT * FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ? ORDER BY started_at DESC, id DESC", (ticket_id,)
   166	    ).fetchall()
   167	    return [run_json(r) for r in rows]
   168	
   169	
   170	def get_run(conn: sqlite3.Connection, run_id: str) -> JsonDict | None:
   171	    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM runs WHERE id = ?", (run_id,)).fetchone()
   172	    return run_json(row) if row is not None else None
   173	
   174	
   175	def list_events_for_entity(conn: sqlite3.Connection, entity_id: str, limit: int) -> list[JsonDict]:
   176	    rows = conn.execute(
   177	        "SELECT id, entity_id, kind, payload, created_at FROM events WHERE entity_id = ? "
   178	        "ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT ?",
   179	        (entity_id, limit),
   180	    ).fetchall()
   181	    return [event_json(r) for r in rows]
   182	
   183	
   184	def copy_text(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str) -> str:
   185	    ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   186	    fields = ticket.fields
   187	
   188	    def show(value: str | None) -> str:
   189	        return value if value else "(none)"
   190	
   191	    link_rows = conn.execute(
   192	        "SELECT from_id, to_id, kind FROM links WHERE from_id = ? OR to_id = ? "
   193	        "ORDER BY kind, from_id, to_id",
   194	        (ticket_id, ticket_id),
   195	    ).fetchall()
   196	    if link_rows:
   197	        links_block = "\n".join(
   198	            f"- {str(r['kind'])}: {str(r['from_id'])} -> {str(r['to_id'])}" for r in link_rows
   199	        )
   200	    else:
   201	        links_block = "(none)"
   202	    return (
   203	        f"{ticket.title}\n"
   204	        f"state: {ticket.state.value}\n"
   205	        f"priority: {ticket.priority.value}\n"
   206	        f"\n"
   207	        f"success:\n{show(fields.success.value)}\n"
   208	        f"\n"
   209	        f"approach:\n{show(fields.approach.value)}\n"
   210	        f"\n"
   211	        f"plan:\n{show(fields.plan.value)}\n"
   212	        f"\n"
   213	        f"result:\n{show(fields.result.value)}\n"
   214	        f"\n"
   215	        f"recap:\n{show(ticket.recap)}\n"
   216	        f"\n"
   217	        f"links:\n{links_block}\n"
   218	    )
   219	
   220	
   221	# --- board (§10.3) -------------------------------------------------------------
   222	
   223	
   224	def board_view(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int) -> JsonDict:
   225	    rows = conn.execute(
   226	        "SELECT id, title, state, priority, deadline, project, fields, claim_lock, "
   227	        "claim_expires, created_at FROM tickets WHERE state != 'dropped'"
   228	    ).fetchall()
   229	    by_state: dict[str, list[tuple[tuple[int, int, str, int], JsonDict]]] = {
   230	        s.value: [] for s in STATE_ORDER
   231	    }
   232	    for row in rows:
   233	        state = str(row["state"])
   234	        priority = str(row["priority"])
   235	        deadline = str(row["deadline"]) if row["deadline"] is not None else None
   236	        fields = json.loads(str(row["fields"]))
   237	        claim_lock = str(row["claim_lock"]) if row["claim_lock"] is not None else None
   238	        claim_expires = int(row["claim_expires"]) if row["claim_expires"] is not None else None
   239	        card: JsonDict = {
   240	            "id": str(row["id"]),
   241	            "title": str(row["title"]),
   242	            "priority": priority,
   243	            "deadline": deadline,
   244	            "project": str(row["project"]) if row["project"] is not None else None,
   245	            "has_pending_proposal": gating_field_pending(TicketState(state), fields),
   246	            "has_running_claim": has_active_claim(claim_lock, claim_expires, now),
   247	        }
   248	        sort_key = (
   249	            _prio_rank(priority),
   250	            0 if deadline is not None else 1,
   251	            deadline or "",
   252	            int(row["created_at"]),
   253	        )
   254	        by_state[state].append((sort_key, card))
   255	    columns: list[JsonDict] = []
   256	    for s in STATE_ORDER:
   257	        cards = [card for _, card in sorted(by_state[s.value], key=lambda item: item[0])]
   258	        columns.append({"state": s.value, "cards": cards})
   259	    return {"columns": columns}
   260

    23	def seed_demo(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    24	    for table in _TABLES:
    25	        if conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {table}").fetchone()[0] > 0:
    26	            raise PlannerError(
    27	                ErrorCode.db_not_empty, "seed --demo requires an empty database", {"table": table}
    28	            )
    29	    now = int(time.time())
    30	    today = datetime.now().astimezone().date()
    31	    conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    32	    try:
    33	        _build_demo(conn, now, today)
    34	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
    35	    except Exception:
    36	        conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
    37	        raise
