   116	def _insert_sprint(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int, today: date) -> str:
   117	    sprint_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["sprint"])
   118	    date_start = (today - timedelta(days=3)).isoformat()
   119	    date_end = (today + timedelta(days=10)).isoformat()
   120	    conn.execute(
   121	        "INSERT INTO sprints (id, name, date_start, date_end, limiting_factor, primary_bet, "
   122	        "supports, premortem, created_at, updated_at) "
   123	        "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
   124	        (
   125	            sprint_id, "Demo Sprint", date_start, date_end,
   126	            "Demo: one clear slice must ship.",
   127	            "Demo: shipping the feature slice unblocks the review queue.",
   128	            "- Demo support line.", "- Demo premortem line.", now, now,
   129	        ),
   130	    )
   131	    append_event(
   132	        conn, sprint_id, EventKind.sprint_created,
   133	        {"name": "Demo Sprint", "date_start": date_start, "date_end": date_end, "source": "demo"},
   134	        now,
   135	    )
   136	    return sprint_id

   164	def _fields(title: str, keys: tuple[str, ...], result_notes: str | None = None) -> str:
   165	    slots: dict[str, dict[str, str | None]] = {
   166	        name: {"value": None, "proposal": None, "notes": None}
   167	        for name in ("success", "approach", "plan", "result")
   168	    }
   169	    for key in keys:
   170	        slots[key]["value"] = f"Demo {key} for {title}"
   171	    if result_notes is not None:
   172	        slots["result"]["notes"] = result_notes
   173	    return json.dumps(slots)

     1	"""§4.4 resolution semantics as pure Decisions. _accept_gating_proposal is the
     2	sole constructor of any Decision that traverses the gating-acceptance edges 1-5
     3	(auto and human alike); _state_change is the sole constructor of state_changed
     4	EventSpecs. Together with data._apply_decision (the sole appender) this is the
     5	"one canonical writer per transition" guarantee (§4.4.6)."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	from typing import Final
    10	
    11	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind
    12	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    13	from planner.tickets.contracts import (
    14	    AtCap,
    15	    FieldName,
    16	    FieldSlot,
    17	    GrantPair,
    18	    NextCeiling,
    19	    Proposal,
    20	    Ticket,
    21	    TicketState,
    22	)
    23	from planner.tickets.logic import admission, fields_codec, machine
    24	from planner.tickets.logic.decisions import Decision, EventSpec
    25	
    26	CAUSE_AUTO_ACCEPT: Final[str] = "auto_accept"
    27	CAUSE_HUMAN_ACCEPT: Final[str] = "human_accept"
    28	CAUSE_REVIEW_APPROVE: Final[str] = "review_approve"
    29	CAUSE_HUMAN_STATE_JUMP: Final[str] = "human_state_jump"
    30	CAUSE_DROP: Final[str] = "drop"
    31	CAUSE_ONWARD_GRANT: Final[str] = "onward_grant"
    32	CAUSE_HUMAN_GRANT: Final[str] = "human_grant"
    33	RESOLVED_BY_AUTO: Final[str] = "auto"
    34	RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN: Final[str] = "human"
    35	
    36	
    37	def _state_change(old: TicketState, new: TicketState, cause: str) -> EventSpec:
    38	    return EventSpec(EventKind.state_changed, {"from": old.value, "to": new.value, "cause": cause})
    39	
    40	
    41	def _accept_gating_proposal(
    42	    ticket: Ticket,
    43	    field: FieldName,
    44	    stored_body: str,
    45	    resolved_by: str,
    46	    edited: bool,
    47	    grant: GrantPair | None,
    48	    cause: str,
    49	    superseded_body: str | None = None,
    50	) -> Decision:
    51	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
    52	    new_slot = FieldSlot(value=stored_body, proposal=None, notes=slot.notes)
    53	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
    54	    new_state = machine.advance_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling)
    55	    events: list[EventSpec] = []
    56	    if superseded_body is not None:
    57	        events.append(
    58	            EventSpec(
    59	                EventKind.proposal_superseded,
    60	                {"field": field.value, "replaced_body": superseded_body},
    61	            )
    62	        )
    63	    events.append(
    64	        EventSpec(
    65	            EventKind.proposal_accepted,
    66	            {
    67	                "field": field.value,
    68	                "body": stored_body,
    69	                "resolved_by": resolved_by,
    70	                "edited": edited,
    71	            },
    72	        )
    73	    )
    74	    events.append(_state_change(ticket.state, new_state, cause))
    75	    new_ceiling: TicketState | None = None
    76	    new_at_cap: AtCap | None = None
    77	    if grant is not None:
    78	        ceiling = grant.next_ceiling
    79	        assert isinstance(ceiling, TicketState)  # resolve_grant always yields a concrete state
    80	        new_ceiling = ceiling
    81	        new_at_cap = grant.at_cap
    82	        events.append(
    83	            EventSpec(
    84	                EventKind.grant_changed,
    85	                {
    86	                    "ceiling": ceiling.value,
    87	                    "at_cap": grant.at_cap.value,
    88	                    "cause": CAUSE_ONWARD_GRANT,
    89	                },
    90	            )
    91	        )
    92	    return Decision(
    93	        events=tuple(events),
    94	        new_fields=new_fields,
    95	        new_state=new_state,
    96	        new_ceiling=new_ceiling,
    97	        new_at_cap=new_at_cap,
    98	    )
    99	
   100	
   101	def decide_file_proposal(
   102	    ticket: Ticket, field: FieldName, body: str, actor: str, now: int
   103	) -> Decision:
   104	    admission.validate_body(body, "proposal body")
   105	    admission.check_agent_proposal(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling, ticket.at_cap, field)
   106	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
   107	    superseded_body = slot.proposal.body if slot.proposal is not None else None
   108	    if machine.auto_accept_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling, field) is not None:
   109	        return _accept_gating_proposal(
   110	            ticket,
   111	            field,
   112	            stored_body=body,
   113	            resolved_by=RESOLVED_BY_AUTO,
   114	            edited=False,
   115	            grant=None,
   116	            cause=CAUSE_AUTO_ACCEPT,
   117	            superseded_body=superseded_body,
   118	        )
   119	    new_slot = FieldSlot(
   120	        value=slot.value,
   121	        proposal=Proposal(body=body, proposed_by=actor, created_at=now),
   122	        notes=slot.notes,
   123	    )
   124	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
   125	    events: list[EventSpec] = []
   126	    if superseded_body is not None:
   127	        events.append(
   128	            EventSpec(
   129	                EventKind.proposal_superseded,
   130	                {"field": field.value, "replaced_body": superseded_body},
   131	            )
   132	        )
   133	    events.append(
   134	        EventSpec(
   135	            EventKind.proposal_filed,
   136	            {"field": field.value, "body": body, "proposed_by": actor},
   137	        )
   138	    )
   139	    return Decision(events=tuple(events), new_fields=new_fields)
   140	
   141	
   142	def decide_accept(
   143	    ticket: Ticket,
   144	    field: FieldName,
   145	    actor: str,
   146	    edited_body: str | None,
   147	    next_ceiling: NextCeiling | None,
   148	    at_cap: AtCap | None,
   149	) -> Decision:
   150	    admission.require_human(actor, "accept_proposal")
   151	    if edited_body is not None:
   152	        admission.validate_body(edited_body, "edit-accept text")
   153	    edited = edited_body is not None
   154	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
   155	    if slot.proposal is None:
   156	        raise PlannerError(
   157	            ErrorCode.not_found,
   158	            "no pending proposal on field",
   159	            {"ticket_id": ticket.id, "field": field.value},
   160	        )
   161	    stored_body = edited_body if edited_body is not None else slot.proposal.body
   162	    if field is machine.gating_field(ticket.state):
   163	        new_state = machine.advance_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling)
   164	        grant = machine.resolve_grant(new_state, next_ceiling, at_cap)
   165	        return _accept_gating_proposal(
   166	            ticket,
   167	            field,
   168	            stored_body=stored_body,
   169	            resolved_by=RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN,
   170	            edited=edited,
   171	            grant=grant,
   172	            cause=CAUSE_HUMAN_ACCEPT,
   173	        )
   174	    new_slot = FieldSlot(value=stored_body, proposal=None, notes=slot.notes)
   175	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
   176	    events = (
   177	        EventSpec(
   178	            EventKind.proposal_accepted,
   179	            {
   180	                "field": field.value,
   181	                "body": stored_body,
   182	                "resolved_by": RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN,
   183	                "edited": edited,
   184	            },
   185	        ),
   186	    )
   187	    return Decision(events=events, new_fields=new_fields)
   188	
   189	
   190	def decide_approve(ticket: Ticket, actor: str) -> Decision:
   191	    admission.require_human(actor, "approve_review")
   192	    if ticket.state is not TicketState.needs_review:
   193	        raise PlannerError(
   194	            ErrorCode.validation,
   195	            "approve requires state needs_review",
   196	            {"state": ticket.state.value},
   197	        )
   198	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, TicketState.done, CAUSE_REVIEW_APPROVE),)
   199	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=TicketState.done)
   200	
   201	
   202	def decide_state_jump(ticket: Ticket, new_state: TicketState, actor: str) -> Decision:
   203	    admission.require_human(actor, "set_state")
   204	    if new_state is TicketState.dropped:
   205	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "use the drop action")
   206	    if ticket.state is TicketState.dropped:
   207	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "dropped is terminal")
   208	    if new_state is ticket.state:
   209	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "ticket already in that state")
   210	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, new_state, CAUSE_HUMAN_STATE_JUMP),)
   211	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=new_state)
   212	
   213	
   214	def decide_drop(ticket: Ticket, actor: str) -> Decision:
   215	    admission.require_human(actor, "drop_ticket")
   216	    if ticket.state is TicketState.done:
   217	        raise PlannerError(
   218	            ErrorCode.validation, "done tickets cannot be dropped", {"state": "done"}
   219	        )
   220	    if ticket.state is TicketState.dropped:
   221	        raise PlannerError(
   222	            ErrorCode.validation, "ticket is already dropped", {"state": "dropped"}
   223	        )
   224	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, TicketState.dropped, CAUSE_DROP),)
   225	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=TicketState.dropped)
   226	
   227	
   228	def decide_grant_change(
   229	    ticket: Ticket, ceiling: TicketState, at_cap: AtCap, actor: str
   230	) -> Decision:
   231	    admission.require_human(actor, "change_grant")
   232	    machine.validate_ceiling(ceiling)
   233	    events = (
   234	        EventSpec(
   235	            EventKind.grant_changed,
   236	            {"ceiling": ceiling.value, "at_cap": at_cap.value, "cause": CAUSE_HUMAN_GRANT},
   237	        ),
   238	    )
   239	    return Decision(events=events, new_ceiling=ceiling, new_at_cap=at_cap)

    20	_ENDPOINT_RULES: Final[dict[LinkKind, tuple[frozenset[str] | None, frozenset[str] | None]]] = {
    21	    LinkKind.belongs_to: (frozenset({"t"}), frozenset({"si"})),
    22	    LinkKind.parent_child: (frozenset({"t"}), frozenset({"t"})),
    23	    LinkKind.blocks: (frozenset({"t"}), frozenset({"t", "si"})),
    24	    LinkKind.relates: (None, None),
    25	}
    26	
    27	
    28	def _prefix(entity_id: str) -> str:
    29	    """The endpoint kind is the id prefix before the first '_' (t_x -> 't')."""
    30	    return entity_id.split("_", 1)[0]
    31	
    32	
    33	def add_link(
    34	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, from_id: str, to_id: str, kind: LinkKind, now: int
    35	) -> None:
    36	    """Create a link, enforcing §3.6: no self-links, fixed endpoint kinds per link,
    37	    at most one belongs_to per ticket, and no blocks/parent_child transitive cycle."""
    38	    detail = {"from_id": from_id, "to_id": to_id, "kind": kind.value}
    39	    # 1. Self-link (§3.6): the two endpoints must differ. Pure check, outside the txn.
    40	    if from_id == to_id:
    41	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.link_invalid, "self-links are not allowed", detail)
    42	    # 2. Endpoint kinds (D8): the prefix fixes each side. Pure check, outside the txn.
    43	    from_rule, to_rule = _ENDPOINT_RULES[kind]
    44	    if from_rule is not None and _prefix(from_id) not in from_rule:
    45	        raise PlannerError(
    46	            ErrorCode.link_invalid,
    47	            f"{kind.value} from-endpoint must be one of {sorted(from_rule)}",
    48	            detail,
    49	        )
    50	    if to_rule is not None and _prefix(to_id) not in to_rule:
    51	        raise PlannerError(
    52	            ErrorCode.link_invalid,
    53	            f"{kind.value} to-endpoint must be one of {sorted(to_rule)}",
    54	            detail,
    55	        )
    56	    # Steps 3–6 serialize under the write lock (A2): BEGIN IMMEDIATE takes the lock
    57	    # before the reads, so two connections cannot interleave BFS-then-insert to admit
    58	    # a cycle or a second belongs_to. Contention resolves via db.connect's busy_timeout.
    59	    own_txn = not conn.in_transaction
    60	    if own_txn:
    61	        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    62	    try:
    63	        # 3. belongs_to uniqueness (§3.6): at most one per ticket.
    64	        if kind is LinkKind.belongs_to:
    65	            existing = conn.execute(
    66	                "SELECT 1 FROM links WHERE from_id=? AND kind='belongs_to' LIMIT 1",
    67	                (from_id,),
    68	            ).fetchone()
    69	            if existing is not None:
    70	                raise PlannerError(
    71	                    ErrorCode.link_invalid,
    72	                    "ticket already has a belongs_to link",
    73	                    {"from_id": from_id},
    74	                )
    75	        # 4. Transitive cycle check (§3.6, D10): from->to makes a cycle iff `from` is
    76	        # reachable from `to` along same-kind edges. BFS from to_id, visited-guarded.
    77	        if kind in _CYCLE_CHECKED:
    78	            queue: deque[str] = deque([to_id])
    79	            visited: set[str] = {to_id}
    80	            while queue:
    81	                node = queue.popleft()
    82	                if node == from_id:
    83	                    raise PlannerError(
    84	                        ErrorCode.link_cycle,
    85	                        f"link would create a {kind.value} cycle",
    86	                        detail,
    87	                    )
    88	                rows = conn.execute(
    89	                    "SELECT to_id FROM links WHERE from_id=? AND kind=?",
    90	                    (node, kind.value),
    91	                ).fetchall()
    92	                for succ in rows:
    93	                    nxt = str(succ["to_id"])
    94	                    if nxt not in visited:
    95	                        visited.add(nxt)
    96	                        queue.append(nxt)
    97	        # 5. Insert; any IntegrityError (PK dup, partial unique index race, CHECK) is
    98	        # re-raised as link_invalid (D9) — a raw IntegrityError never escapes.
    99	        try:
   100	            conn.execute(
   101	                "INSERT INTO links (from_id, to_id, kind) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
   102	                (from_id, to_id, kind.value),
   103	            )
   104	        except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:
   105	            raise PlannerError(
   106	                ErrorCode.link_invalid,
   107	                "link already exists or violates a link constraint",
   108	                detail,
   109	            ) from exc
   110	        # 6. Event.
   111	        append_event(conn, from_id, EventKind.link_added, detail, now)
   112	    except BaseException:
   113	        if own_txn:
   114	            conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
   115	        raise
   116	    if own_txn:
   117	        conn.execute("COMMIT")
   118	
   119	
   120	def remove_link(
   121	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, from_id: str, to_id: str, kind: LinkKind, now: int
   122	) -> None:
   123	    """Delete a link; a missing link is not_found (D12)."""
   124	    detail = {"from_id": from_id, "to_id": to_id, "kind": kind.value}
   125	    cursor = conn.execute(
   126	        "DELETE FROM links WHERE from_id=? AND to_id=? AND kind=?",
   127	        (from_id, to_id, kind.value),
   128	    )
   129	    if cursor.rowcount == 0:
   130	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.not_found, "link not found", detail)
   131	    append_event(conn, from_id, EventKind.link_removed, detail, now)
   132	
   133	
   134	def blocked_target_ids(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> set[str]:
   135	    """Ids (tickets or sprint items) currently blocked: targets of a blocks link
   136	    whose source ticket is not done/dropped (§3.6 line 57)."""
   137	    rows = conn.execute(
   138	        "SELECT DISTINCT l.to_id FROM links l "
   139	        "JOIN tickets src ON src.id = l.from_id "
   140	        "WHERE l.kind = 'blocks' AND src.state NOT IN ('done', 'dropped')"
   141	    ).fetchall()
   142	    return {str(row["to_id"]) for row in rows}
   143	
   144	
   145	def is_blocked(conn: sqlite3.Connection, entity_id: str) -> bool:
   146	    """Whether one entity is blocked. The join to tickets makes the rule literal:
   147	    only a ticket source in an open state blocks; a missing/non-ticket source never
   148	    does (D8)."""
   149	    row = conn.execute(
   150	        "SELECT 1 FROM links l "
   151	        "JOIN tickets src ON src.id = l.from_id "
   152	        "WHERE l.kind = 'blocks' AND src.state NOT IN ('done', 'dropped') "
   153	        "AND l.to_id = ? LIMIT 1",
   154	        (entity_id,),
   155	    ).fetchone()
   156	    return row is not None

    20	  function appendChildren(parent, children) {
    21	    if (children === null || children === undefined) {
    22	      return;
    23	    }
    24	    if (Array.isArray(children)) {
    25	      children.forEach(function (child) {
    26	        if (child) {
    27	          parent.appendChild(child);
    28	        }
    29	      });
    30	    } else {
    31	      parent.appendChild(children);
    32	    }
    33	  }

   210	**(9) Copy ticket** — `C.panel("Copy", [copyButton])`. `copyButton` = `<button class="button" data-copy>` "Copy ticket". On click, in-flight:
   211	```
   212	fetchText("/api/tickets/"+id+"/copy-text")          // §7.6? no — plain text; MUST NOT use fetchJson (JSON.parse would reject text)
   213	  .then(copyToClipboard)                             // clipboard write
   214	  .then(function(){ flash button text → "Copied", restore after a short timeout },
   215	        function(err){ errorHost.prepend(C.errorLine(err)); })
   216	```
   217	`copy-text` returns `text/plain` (`PlainTextResponse`, tickets/api.py 424–426 → `copy_text`, views.py 184–218: title / state / priority / success / approach / plan / result / recap / links block). This is a read, not a WS-mutating action; no flush follows, so the "Copied" flash is the only feedback and is set directly (the sole sanctioned direct DOM tweak in the ticket screen; documented as such).
   218	
   219	**(10) Links** — `C.panel("Links", [linksList, addForm])`. `linksList` `[data-links]`:
   220	- Per `detail.links` entry `{from_id,to_id,kind}` → a row `[data-link-row][data-link-kind=<kind>]`:
   221	  - text: `kind` · `from_id` " → " far-end. Far end = the id that is not `id`; if it starts with `"t_"` render it as `<a href="#/ticket/"+farId>` (both `from_id` and `to_id` shown; only ticket ids get anchors — sprint-item/other ids render as plain text).
   222	  - Remove button → `DELETE /api/links?from_id=<>&to_id=<>&kind=<>` (query params, remove_link, tickets/api.py 438–444). Build the query with `encodeURIComponent`.
   223	- `addForm` `[data-link-add]`: `from_id` text input (prefilled with `id`), `to_id` text input, `kind` `<select>` over `LINK_KINDS`, Add button → `POST /api/links` body `{from_id, to_id, kind}` (add_link, tickets/api.py 429–435).
   224	
   225	**(11) Day / sprint assignment** —
   226	- Day `[data-day-assign]`: date `<input type="date">` defaulted to the client's today (`new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)`) + "Add to day" button → `POST /api/day/{isoDate}/tickets` body `{ticket_id: id}` (days/api.py 129–137; `{date}` accepts ISO or `today`; client sends ISO). Existing `detail.day_ids` each a row `[data-day-row]` showing the day id + Remove → `DELETE /api/day/{date}/tickets/{id}` where **`date = dayId.slice("day_".length)`** i.e. `dayId.slice(4)` (ids.py 30–31: `day_id` = `"day_" + isoDate`; e.g. `day_2026-07-05` → `2026-07-05`).
   227	- Sprint `[data-sprint-assign]`: if `detail.sprint_item_id != null` → plain text `"sprint: " + (detail.effective_sprint_id || "(none)")`, **no select** (server rejects `set_sprint` on parented tickets — `_set_project`/`set_sprint` guard, and sprint is derived through the item). Else → `<select>` over `sprints.sprints` (option value = `s.id`, label = `s.name`) plus a leading `"(none)"` option (value `""`), preselected to `detail.sprint_id`; Save → `PATCH /api/tickets/{id}` body `{sprint_id: value || null}` (patch_ticket, tickets/api.py 263–288).
   228	
   229	**(12) Run history** — `C.panel("Runs", [ C.runHistory(runs.runs) ])` (D11 #15).
   230	
   231	**(13) Event log** — `C.panel("Events", [ C.eventLog(evs.events) ])` (D11 #14).
   232	
   233	**(14) Chat** — `C.panel("Chat", [chatWrap])`. `chatWrap` = `<div data-chat>` containing `C.chatPanel({ entityId: id })` (T15, §7). The `[data-chat]` wrapper is T16's; T15's component owns its internal DOM/attributes.
   234	
   235	### 3.3 Private helpers in `screens-ticket.js`
   236	
   237	- `marker(v)` — same as board (`chip` + `data-marker`).
   238	- `fetchText(path)` — `return fetch(path).then(function(r){ if (!r.ok) return r.text().then(function(t){ throw errorFrom(r, t); }); return r.text(); });` — a tiny text fetch so `copy-text` is not run through `fetchJson`. (Reject shape need only carry `.code`/`.message` for `errorLine`; a minimal `{code:"http_error", message:"HTTP "+status}` is fine — do **not** modify api.js.)
   239	- `copyToClipboard(text)` — `if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) return navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);` else fallback: create a hidden `<textarea>`, set value, append, `select()`, `document.execCommand("copy")`, remove; resolve/reject a Promise accordingly.
   240	- `submit(button, errorHost, fn)` — the in-flight pattern for the link/day/sprint/copy buttons (same shape as `inFlight` in components.js; screens carry their own copy since the components helper is private to that file).
   241	- `selectEl(options, selectedValue)` — builds a `<select>` from `[{value,label}]`, preselecting `selectedValue`.
   242

   245	## 4. `assets/app.css` — additive sections (tokens-only)
   246	
   247	Append new blocks; every color/size/duration/font resolves through a `var(--token)` from `tokens.css` (permitted non-token literals: `0`, unitless ratios, percentages/viewport units, keywords, font-weights — matching app.css's own header rule, lines 1–4). New class names follow the kebab convention: `board-*`, `ticket-*`, `state-control-*`, `grant-control-*`, `run-history-*`, `event-log-*`.
   248	
   249	- **Board** — `.board { display: flex; gap: var(--space-4); overflow-x: auto; }` (horizontal scroll, constraint 8); `.board-column { min-width: calc(var(--space-7) * 5); display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--space-2); }` (min-width = an existing space-token multiple; ≈240px); `.board-column-head { … }` (uses `--type-sm`, `--text-muted`, spacing tokens); `.board-column-cards { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--space-1); }`.
   250	- **Ticket** — single-column stack of `.panel`s (the panels already `margin-bottom: var(--space-4)`); no two-column layout (simplicity, constraint 8). `.ticket-header-chips { display: flex; gap: var(--space-1); flex-wrap: wrap; }`.
   251	- **State control** — `.state-control { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-2); flex-wrap: wrap; }` + `.state-control-select`/error styling from tokens.
   252	- **Grant control** — `.grant-control { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-3); flex-wrap: wrap; }`; `.grant-control-atcap { display: flex; gap: var(--space-2); }`.
   253	- **Run history / Event log** — row layouts: `.run-history-row`, `.event-log-row { display: flex; gap: var(--space-3); … }`; `.run-history-status`/`.event-log-kind` mono via `--font-mono`, `--type-xs`; times `--text-faint`.
   254	- **Links / day / sprint** — `.ticket-link-row`, `.ticket-day-row`, `.link-add`, `.day-assign`, `.sprint-assign` as flex rows with `--space-*` gaps.
   255	- Native `<select>`/`<input type="date"|"text">` inherit the existing `button, textarea { font: inherit; }` where relevant; add a shared `.select`/input rule if needed, tokens-only, mirroring `.field-editor-input` (app.css 200–216).
   256	
   257	No new token category is introduced (PRINCIPLES.md "New token categories require evidence of need"). If a genuinely new themable value is unavoidable, that is a blocker to raise, not an inline literal.

   261	## 5. `orchestration/tickets/T16-board-ticket/smoke.py`
   262	
   263	Model directly on `orchestration/tickets/T14-ui-foundation/smoke.py`: in-process uvicorn on a daemon thread, temp DB, `free_or(PORT)`, `wait_ready`, `ok NN —` numbered prints, `try/finally` teardown (close client/page/browser, stop playwright, `server.should_exit`, join, rmtree), final `SMOKE PASS (N checks)`, any failed assertion → traceback + exit 1.
   264	
   265	**Differences from T14's env (constraint 7):**
   266	- `PLAN_TEST_MODE = "1"` but **NO `PLAN_FAKE_NOW`** — `seed_demo` stamps real wall-clock (`time.time()`, `datetime.now()`, seed/demo.py 29–30); a fake clock would skew the day/deadline alignment the demo builds against `today`.
   267	- Keep `PLAN_DB_PATH`, `PLAN_PORT`, `PLAN_LOGS_DIR`, `PLAN_DISPATCHER_LOCK_PATH`, `PLAN_WS_POLL_MS="50"` as in T14 (tight flush cadence; ui_debounce stays 250).
   268	- Gateway resolves to the **echo fake** by default in test mode (`gateway_adapter` default `"auto"` → `fake` → `EchoGatewayAdapter`, online, `available=True`, replies `"echo: <text>"`; registry.py 61–69, fakes.py 86–99). So the chat assertion follows the **online** branch.
   269	
   270	**Setup (before the browser drives anything):**
   271	1. Boot server, `wait_ready`. Seed: `client.post("/api/seed", json={"demo": True})` → 200 (seed/api.py 37–58; requires empty DB, which the temp DB is).
   272	2. `client.get("/api/tickets")` → map title → id. Capture:
   273	   - `t_success` = "Draft the onboarding email success criteria." (needs_success, ceiling needs_success, at_cap propose)
   274	   - `t_impl`    = "Implement the demo feature slice." (in_progress, P0)
   275	   - `t_flaky`   = "Fix the flaky login test." (in_progress, P0)
   276	   - `t_review`  = "Review the analytics dashboard numbers." (needs_review — for the relates target)
   277	   - `t_dropped` = "Prototype the voice input toggle." (dropped — must be absent from the board)
   278	3. **(a) Park a pending proposal** on `t_success`: `client.post("/api/tickets/"+t_success+"/propose/success", json={"body":"Smoke success body."}, headers={"X-Plan-Actor":"agent"})` → 200. Plain-agent context (actor "agent", no claim → `is_claimed_agent` False, no `require_claim`; authctx 57–65). At ceiling `needs_success` with `at_cap=propose`, `success` is the gating field, advance-target `needs_approach` > ceiling → proposal parks (§4.4.2–3). Board `has_pending_proposal` = `gating_field_pending` → true.
   279	4. **(b) Live claim + two runs** on `t_impl`, via direct sqlite (sanctioned — smoke owns the temp DB, dispatcher out of scope): open `sqlite3.connect(db_path)`; `UPDATE tickets SET claim_lock='claim_smoke01', claim_expires=<now+3600> WHERE id=?`; `INSERT INTO runs (id,ticket_id,status,started_at,ended_at,summary,error,pid) VALUES ('run_smoke_run',?,'running',<now-120>,NULL,NULL,NULL,NULL)` and `('run_smoke_done',?,'done',<now-600>,<now-300>,'demo done run',NULL,NULL)` (columns per db.py 128–138; status ∈ the CHECK set). commit, close. Board `has_running_claim` for `t_impl` = `has_active_claim` → true.
   280	5. **(c) Auto-block** `t_flaky`: `UPDATE tickets SET auto_blocked=1 WHERE id=?`, commit. (For the unblock round-trip; `t_flaky` is a `blocks` **source**, so it is not itself link-blocked.)
   281	6. Launch chromium; **grant clipboard perms** on the context: `context.grant_permissions(["clipboard-read","clipboard-write"])` (Playwright: create context, then page). `page.goto(base + "/")`, wait for `window.__plannerDebug.wsOpens >= 1`.
   282	
   283	**Assertions (Playwright DOM against `#/board` and `#/ticket/<id>`; number every one):**
   284	
   285	1. **Board columns + counts + dropped hidden.** goto `#/board`; wait for `[data-screen="board"]`. Assert exactly six `[data-column]`, in order `needs_success, needs_approach, needs_plan, in_progress, needs_review, done`. Card counts: needs_success 1, needs_approach 1, needs_plan 1, in_progress 2, needs_review 1, done 1. Assert `t_dropped`'s title text appears in **no** `[data-card]`.
   286	2. **Markers + card chips.** The `t_success` card (`[data-card][data-ticket-id=t_success]`) carries `[data-marker="pending-proposal"]`; the `t_impl` card carries `[data-marker="running-claim"]`. A card shows priority/deadline/project chips (assert the `t_success` card has a priority chip, a deadline chip, a project chip present).
   287	3. **Navigate via card click.** Click the `t_success` card; wait for `location.hash === "#/ticket/"+t_success`. Assert `[data-screen="ticket"][data-ticket-id=t_success]`, `[data-state="needs_success"]`, and the `[data-field="success"]` section hosts the proposal card (T15's proposal element — see §7 assumed attribute `[data-proposal-accept]` inside `[data-field="success"]`).
   288	4. **Accept-with-grant from the ticket screen.** Inside `[data-field="success"]`, use T15's grant-pair picker (assumed `[data-grant-pick-ceiling]` select + `[data-grant-pick-at-cap]` radios, §7): choose a `next_ceiling` (e.g. `needs_approach`) and an `at_cap` (e.g. `propose`), click `[data-proposal-accept]`. Wait for the flush re-render; assert `[data-state="needs_approach"]` and the `success` `[data-field]` markdown block now shows "Smoke success body." (value ← proposal body; advance one step, §4.4.4/§4.4.7; `advance_target(needs_success, …)` = `needs_approach`, machine.py 40–48).
   289	5. **Copy.** Click `[data-copy]`. Read the clipboard via `page.evaluate("navigator.clipboard.readText()")` and assert it equals `client.get("/api/tickets/"+t_success+"/copy-text").text` byte-for-byte. **Fallback** (if headless clipboard read returns empty/blocked): assert the `[data-copy]` button text became "Copied" AND that the direct `copy-text` GET returns the expected block (title line + `state:` + `priority:` + field sections + `recap:` + `links:`), proving the data path even when the browser clipboard is unreadable.
   290	6. **Links add/remove.** In `[data-link-add]`: set `to_id` = `t_flaky`, kind = `relates`, click Add. Wait for flush; assert a `[data-link-row][data-link-kind="relates"]` appears whose text references `t_flaky`. Click that row's Remove; wait for flush; assert the `relates` row is gone.
   291	7. **Run history + event log.** goto `#/ticket/`+`t_impl`; assert `[data-run-history]` has exactly two `[data-run-row]`, one `[data-run-status="running"]` and one `[data-run-status="done"]`. Then (event log — asserted on `t_success`, the accepted ticket) goto `#/ticket/`+`t_success`; assert `[data-event-log]` contains a `[data-event-row][data-event-kind="ticket_created"]` and rows `[data-event-kind="proposal_accepted"]` and `[data-event-kind="state_changed"]` (the accept from item 4). (Runs live on `t_impl`; the accept events live on `t_success` — the two halves are asserted on their respective screens.)
   292	8. **Grant control.** On `t_success`: set `[data-grant-ceiling]` = `needs_plan`, `[data-grant-at-cap]` = `stop`, click `[data-grant-save]`. Wait for flush; assert the re-rendered `[data-grant-ceiling]` value is `needs_plan` and the `stop` radio is checked (grant persisted, `change_grant`).
   293	9. **State control + unblock + drop.** On `t_success`: set `[data-state-select]` = `needs_plan`, click `[data-state-jump]`; wait for flush; assert `[data-state="needs_plan"]`. Then goto `#/ticket/`+`t_flaky`; assert an auto-blocked marker (`[data-marker="auto-blocked"]`) and `[data-unblock]` present; click `[data-unblock]`; wait for flush; assert the auto-blocked marker is gone. Then goto `#/ticket/`+`t_success` (now `needs_plan`), click `[data-drop]`; wait for flush; goto `#/board`; assert no `[data-card][data-ticket-id=t_success]` in any column (it dropped out of the `needs_plan` column it sat in after the jump).
   294	10. **Chat.** goto `#/ticket/`+`t_impl` (a live ticket); assert `[data-chat]` present. Minimal exercise against the online echo fake: locate the first `textarea`/`input` inside `[data-chat]`, fill "smoke hello", click the panel's send control, wait for the text "echo: smoke hello" to appear inside `[data-chat]`. **Fallback** (T15 internal DOM differs / control not found): assert `[data-chat]` present AND `client.get("/api/chat/"+t_impl+"/status").json()["available"] is True` (proves the online path; chat/api.py 38–43, echo fake `available=True`).
   295	
   296	Print `SMOKE PASS (N checks)` and return 0.

   324	## 7. T15 interface assumptions (EXTERNAL CONTRACTS — reconcile against T15's landed code before/at integration)
   325	
   326	T15 owns D11 items 7 (proposal card) + 8 (grant-pair picker) + 10 (chat panel) and adds them to `Planner.components`. T16 calls them; it does not define them. The assumed signatures below let T16 be written now; when T15 lands, the diff to reality must be mechanical (rename a key / an attribute), not structural. If a mismatch is structural, that is an integration blocker to log, not to paper over.
   327	
   328	**`Planner.components.proposalCard(opts) → DOM node`** — assumed:
   329	- `opts.proposal` = the `fields[field].proposal` object `{body, proposed_by, created_at}`.
   330	- `opts.field` = the field name string (label / disambiguation).
